**Tests** (each part, as AGENTS.md asks)

- Domain: the age check, the house marker, the weekly sign-up cap over the workspace's week, Free's renewal dates (half-open, each row following the last), the tier the house's plans that grant today make, a workout of one's own (an exercise in use in the house's pool, typed weights only, frozen once finished), the limit's window, one Pro+ program at a time, a block that would end past its pass refused, the day a plan is booked from when its money is recorded, Max's places, anonymising keeps the facts; fixed reference tests for any rule with a version name.
- Integration against PostgreSQL: an anonymous sign-up with the guards (the honeypot kept nowhere, the stamp, the limits, the weekly cap), antiforgery refused, the confirmation link, the consumer terms asked only there, the sweep; unauthorized (an anonymous caller on client routes, a trainee on every staff route, a trainee on another trainee's rows); wrong tenant (a sign-up cannot land in a coach's workspace, a trainee's reads under another workspace's `X-Tenant-Id`, a coach's offer cannot be booked by a trainee, a coach's workspace never sees a house profile); constraints (the GiST overlap untouched by Free beside a paid plan and still refusing two paid plans at once, a booking racing the Free renewal, the append-only triggers on payments, maxes and asks, the workout triggers still refusing a delete or a rewrite of a program's workout); concurrency (two sign-ups with one email, two asks at once, one ask booked twice, the last Max place asked for twice, idempotency keys bound to payloads); the read-only allow list unchanged; health fields absent from every log line (`CapturedTestLog`). Extend `A11aSignUpTests`, `A16aLegalConsentTests`, `Phase1WorkflowTests`, `Phase2CommercialWorkflowTests`, `Step3RenewalRequestTests`, `Phase3TrainingWorkflowTests`, `P2TrainingMaxesTests`, `ReleaseClientTests`, `CoachDepartureAndClientReturnTests`, `PlatformBillingTests`, `R62gTalkToUsTests`, `Phase5B5MediaQuotaTests`, and `Phase4NutritionHardeningTests` for Max's meal plan; architecture `ModuleDependencyTests` and `TenancyModelInvariantTests` for any new module reference or tenant-owned table. Each part that changes server code runs the backend tests of section 1, and the runner's final check runs the whole suite.
- Integration, for what changed on 11 Nov 2026: the pool (a house trainee reads the house's exercises in use and opens their demos, never an archived one and never another workspace's, and writes none; without Training access nothing is read; a coach's client still reads no library, `B9ExerciseLibraryTests` unchanged); a workout of one's own (started, an exercise added, sets saved, finished, then read in history, records and "last time"; another trainee's is 404; refused without Training access; frozen once finished); Free beside a paid plan (Training granted while an ask waits and after a pass ends; a program's sessions refused once its pass no longer grants); an ask books nothing, and one step books the plan and records its receipt.
- Vitest: the `/train` page and its full-week line, the sign-up form and its reasons, Today's self-directed card with Start a workout and Carry on, the pool's page and its looping clip, the player for a workout of one's own (add an exercise, log, finish), the limit's sentence, the programs page with Ask for Pro+ and Start, Ask for a plan and I've paid, Max's places, the Health step skipped, Me's Close account.
- Mocked Playwright: `e2e/auth.e2e.ts`, `legal.e2e.ts`, `home.e2e.ts`, `client-today.e2e.ts`, `client-training.e2e.ts`, `me.e2e.ts`, `client-service.e2e.ts` extended; a new `train.e2e.ts`.
- Live, on a scratch demo with a house: `e2e/live/register-and-confirm.e2e.ts`, `invite-and-onboard.e2e.ts`, `sell-and-pay.e2e.ts`, `update-maxes.e2e.ts`, `security-tenancy.e2e.ts` and `unpaid-read-only.e2e.ts` as models; a trainee signs up, confirms, opens the pool, logs a workout of their own, asks for Pro+, keeps training on Free until the house's staff record the payment, then starts a block with their own max; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, no sideways scroll, 200% text.

**Left out** (and why)

- One-to-one coaching: not a tier of the house. Decision 18 (7 Nov 2026) puts no promise about it in the founding letter, and the trainer partner will sell one-to-one sessions; how they are sold, and the later paid consultation in 30, are later steps, not this row. The trainer partner is not in the turn when a trainee asks for a coach (decision 4).
- Own routines and Pro (28, 32), the Find a coach list (35, plan Step 4), the shared log (Step 6): later steps, each a row of its own when the owner opens it.
- A new max a trainee's logged sets suggest, accepted with one tap: needs the owner, who took suggested maxes out of the app (decision 19's note, 7 Nov 2026; N10a and N10b took them out, N10b with their rule, BestLoggedSet v1, which git and ADR 0036 keep).
- A readiness screen for people training without a coach: only if the lawyer says so; TB Gym's own questions, written with a qualified reviewer.
- The front-desk role (C8 slice 2) and its two small choices.
- Any price, discount, refund, cancellation-window or retention rule, and every legal wording: needs the owner and the lawyer; never written in code or docs.
- Buying the 3D motion library and reading its licence: the owner's step, after its free sample is tried (C20; 12 Nov 2026: wanted, not bought yet). N9b4 builds the looping clip and N9b6 the command that loads the library; when the pool opens to strangers is the owner's, since sign-up is theirs to turn on.
- Saved routines and a routine builder for trainees: Step 5 (28). A workout of one's own is logged, not saved to be started again.
- The pool or a workout of one's own in a coach's space: a coach's clients train on what their coach gave them, and MED-002's library grant is built for the house alone.
- A trainee's own exercises: nobody but the house's staff adds to the pool.
- A graph of one lift for a trainee: no client has one today.
- Point 6's self-guided drop rule for programs nobody watches, Point 3's in-app goal form and AI suggestions, Meta's API (decision 38, 7 Nov 2026: later, once 3 coaches ask), Whish automation (Step 5), card payments, holding money for coaches (C17).
- Code for the test itself: Step 2 runs by hand on what exists.

**Open** (12 Nov 2026: the owner asked that only a question that blocks a build be put to them. Two did, when to start and how far to go, and both were answered that day. Nothing below holds up a row; each line says where it stands.)

Answered by the owner on 12 Nov 2026:

- When the build starts: now. Step 2's test runs beside it on today's app and no longer decides whether the first version is built.
- How far it goes: every part, Pro+ and Max included, built and kept switched off until the owner turns each on (Rules).
- The 3D motion library: wanted, "not a maybe", and not bought yet. N9b4 plays a clip the animated way and N9b6 loads the library the day its files exist; which of C20's two is bought, and when, is the owner's.

Read from the owners' earlier answers, and told to the owner on 12 Nov 2026:

- The limit on Free (27): the owners ticked a window on Progress's charts, and every logged workout stays readable (N9b5); the window's length is a setting. The other way, this row's first reading, locks older workouts.
- Own routines (28): only if testers ask; they are Step 5, with Pro (32). The order of Step 5 against Step 4 is asked once N9's rows are built.
- Who sees the trainees (33): both partners; once N9d1 is built the Owner sees the business side and the Coach the private side (C8, decided 14 Oct 2026).
- Max beside the coaches (34, and the owner's word of 11 Nov 2026): Max is wanted, and a coach's own client may buy the house's plans. What TB Gym says to a founding coach who objects in Step 1's calls is the partners', outside the build.

Built as the default, told to the owner on 12 Nov 2026 without objection; each row lists its own choices in section 3:

- Sign-up is built switched off (N9a1). The owner turns it on, so whether strangers join before the lawyer's consumer terms are published is the owner's call on that day, with the lawyer (decision 2 settles only the health consent).
- A self-directed trainee's Today (25's note): "Self-directed · TB Gym coaching space", no Message link, nothing on Coach Today (N9a1, N9a2).
- Starting a workout from a past one: yes (N9b2, N9b3). The other way: nothing until routines (28).
- The coaches' homepage: no link to `/train` (N9a2). The other way: one quiet link.
- A part payment (N9c1): nothing is booked until the exact price has come, the rule coaching spaces already keep (SUB-008). Booking on a part would be a new money rule, which no chat writes.
- A program longer than a pass (N9c3): blocks, each ending inside its pass. The pass's length against a block's is set when the house's staff make the offer (SUB-002 sells a length in weeks or in days); a pass exactly as long as a block leaves no day to start late. The other ways, a pass as long as the program or a program across passes, need a domain decision.
- The monthly check (29's note): which features Pro+'s offer covers is the house's staff's to tick when they make it (the trainer partner's answer puts a monthly check with Pro+); no code differs either way.

Settings the owner fills in the app, each empty until then, never in code, a seed or a doc: new sign-ups a week and the age that may sign up (N9a1); the window of Free's Progress and the house's storage allowance (N9b5); the house's Whish number (N9c1); Max's places (N9c5); every price, length and plan word (the house's offers); the retention periods (N9e, the lawyer's list). Whether the house is ever billed on the platform plan is a discount the platform admin sets.

The owners', outside the build:

- The test's pass mark (Step 2), and what the test counts while no tester can pay.
- What a month of Max holds (how often the program is looked at again, how soon a message is answered, what the monthly check asks), written before Max is turned on.
- The company and the lawyer's texts: no trainee pays before the company's Whish account exists (3, 13); the consumer terms, the privacy wording, the age line and the cancellation window are the lawyer's.
- Buying the 3D library and reading its licence (C20).

### N9a1 · The house and trainee sign-up, the server

**Follows.** Decisions 24, 25, 33, 34 and 36, decision 2, and the owner's word of 12 Nov 2026 (N9's Follows): the build starts now and sign-up is built switched off. The first row of N9; nothing of N9 exists before it.

**Builds.** An outline; the chat that opens this row writes its spec from N9's part "N9a" (its server half), N9's Rules, Where and Tests, and the code as it stands, as N6d's chat did from N6c. Server only. (1) The house: one workspace is marked as the house, at most one (default: a column on the tenant row set by a `platform-admin` command beside `grant`). (2) The house's settings: data the platform admin enters through an admin API, each change kept with who made it and when, each empty until filled. This row adds the two it needs, new sign-ups a week and the age that may sign up; later rows add theirs. (3) `POST /api/auth/register/trainee`, as N9a says: anonymous, antiforgery, the public-authentication rate limit and one of its own, Talk to us's honeypot and stamp, the age check before any account is stored, the week's sign-ups counted over the workspace's week, an unconfirmed account with the coach sign-up's confirmation mail, a Client membership and a `ClientProfile` in the house assigned to the house's Coach and marked self-directed, and the acceptance of every document in force for a house trainee in the same transaction, the consumer terms among them as a new `LegalDocumentKind` the platform admin publishes and nothing seeds. An email that already has an account signs in first and gains only the membership and the profile (34). (4) Switched off (12 Nov 2026): while no workspace is the house, or its sign-ups a week is empty or zero, the route refuses with a reason of its own and stores nothing; a public read tells `/train` whether sign-up is open, full for the week or closed. (5) A Worker sweep removes house sign-ups never confirmed after a set number of days, and nothing else. (6) Self-directed: a trainee marked so raises nothing on Coach Today (the attention policy moves to its next version with its reference tests), and their workout notes and leavings reach no staff inbox; both partners keep them on Clients (33). (7) Docs: a one-page ADR for the house (which module owns the marker and the sign-up; default Tenancy, the orchestration beside `RegisterCoachAsync`), ADR 0002 and DOMAIN-RULES section 11 amended, the new rules numbered.

**Rules.** N9's, all of them. No legal wording is written or seeded: the consumer terms, the privacy wording and the age line's sentence are the lawyer's, published by the platform admin (CLI-010). With no document published for a house trainee the sign-up asks for none, as a coach's does today; whether sign-up is turned on before they exist is the owner's (N9's Open). The honeypot's value is kept nowhere. A sign-up lands only in the house, never in a coach's workspace, whatever the request carries.

**Where.** N9's Where: Joining, Spam guards, Tenancy and scope, Clients, Legal, Worker, Docs.

**Tests.** Domain: the age check, the marker, the week's count over the workspace's week. Integration against PostgreSQL: the guards (the honeypot kept nowhere, the stamp, the limits), antiforgery refused, closed while the setting is empty, the confirmation link, the documents asked only there, two sign-ups with one email, the last place of the week asked for twice, a trainee on every staff route, a sign-up that cannot land in a coach's workspace, another workspace's `X-Tenant-Id`, the sweep, the settings' API refused to anyone but the platform admin, no health field in a log line. The attention policy's reference tests. The architecture tests if a module gains a dependency.

**Left out.** Every screen (N9a2). Free's plan and the pool (N9b1): until then a confirmed trainee has no plan, which only a test reaches, since sign-up is closed. The wording of any document or of the age line. A waitlist when the week is full.

**Open.** Where the house's settings live (default: a table of the Tenancy module, one row per change, the latest in force; the other way: a configuration key, which needs a deployment to change and breaks the owner's rule that figures are entered as data). Whether empty and zero both mean closed (default: yes). The sweep's days (default: a configuration value beside the confirmation mail's lifetime; it is housekeeping, not a figure the owner sets).

**As built** (12 Nov 2026; ADR 0041, CLI-022 to CLI-026; what N9a2 and N9b1 build on). The mark is `tenancy."Tenants"."IsHouse"`, set by `platform-admin house set <slug or id>`, shown by `platform-admin house` and cleared by `house clear` while the house holds nobody. The admin's API: `GET /api/platform-admin/house` (the house, each setting with its value, version, who changed it and when, and where sign-up stands: status, the week's days, places taken, sign-ups waiting) and `PUT /api/platform-admin/house/settings/{SignUpsPerWeek|MinimumSignUpAge}` with `{ value, expectedVersion }` (400 under `value`, 409 `house_setting_changed`, 409 `house_not_set`). The public read `GET /api/auth/register/trainee` answers `status` Open, Full or Closed and, while open or full, the house's name, its time zone, the age, `opensAgainOn` when full, `formStamp` when open and, for someone signed in, `callerSignUp` (Joined, or Waiting for a place). The form `POST /api/auth/register/trainee` takes `{ firstName, lastName, email, birthDate, password, formStamp, website (the honeypot), legalDocumentIds }`: 202 for a new account, whose confirmation email is a coach's, and 200 with `joined` for someone signed in. Its refusals by code: `trainee_sign_up_closed`, `trainee_sign_up_full` (with `opensAgainOn`), `trainee_sign_up_age_not_met` (with `minimumAge`), `sign_up_form_too_fast`, `sign_up_form_stamp_invalid`, `existing_account_sign_in_required`, `wrong_signed_in_account`, `trainee_already_joined`, `trainee_former_staff`, `trainee_sign_up_not_available`, `trainee_sign_up_once_a_week`, `legal_acceptance_required`, and the validation keys `firstName`, `lastName`, `email`, `birthDate`, `password`, `identity`. Where it differs from the outline above, each with its reason in section 3: the membership and the client record are created, and the week's place taken, when the email is confirmed (`POST /api/auth/confirm-email`, unchanged for the page), so N9b1 starts Free in `TraineeSignUpService.CompleteAsync` and in its signed-in join; a confirmation into a week that has filled leaves the person confirmed and waiting; the sweep empties the never-confirmed account and deletes its sign-up, not the account's row. A trainee's own read (`GET /api/client-profile/me`) and the staff's list row carry `selfDirected`, and the documents a trainee ticks come from `GET /api/legal/documents/to-accept?audience=Trainee`. On the web only the admin's Legal documents page (the consumer terms) and the coach history's "signed up" changed; the generated client holds every type above.
### N9a2 · Trainee sign-up on screen

**Follows.** N9a1's API. Decision 25 and its note, as the owner was told on 12 Nov 2026 (a self-directed trainee's screens name the house, not a coach), 33, 34; the plan's card 34 for the homepage.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9a" (its screens) and from N9a1 as built. Web only. The public `/train` page in the signed-out frame and TB Gym's own colours (`HOUSE_BRAND`): the house's offer in a few lines, "Train with TB Gym", one button, "Create your account", and the line that training days follow the house's clock. While sign-up is closed the page says so and offers no form; while the week is full it reads "Sign-up is full for this week. It opens again on Monday." The sign-up form on the model of `features/auth/register-coach.ts` (name, email, password, date of birth, a box for each document in force with the lawyer's sentence beside it, none when none is published), each refusal in words, and the confirmation screen. The homepage's clients sentence, "Clients join by invitation from their coach, never from a public sign-up.", loses its second half and gains no link. The platform admin's House page: which workspace is the house, and its settings with who last changed each and when. A self-directed trainee's Today and Me: the coach card reads "Self-directed · TB Gym coaching space" with no Message link, and Training's "Your coach will put your program here." gives way to a line that fits a trainee until N9b3 adds Start a workout.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md's kit, words and definition of done. Nothing here decides who may sign up: the server does (N9a1). `/train` is a lazy route and adds nothing to the first download.

**Where.** N9's Where: Joining (the Angular half); `core/theme/redesigned-route.ts`, `features/home/home-content.ts`, `features/dashboard/client-today.html`, `features/platform-admin/`.

**Tests.** Vitest: `/train` in its three states, the form and each refusal, Today's self-directed card, the House page. Mocked Playwright: `auth.e2e.ts`, `legal.e2e.ts`, `home.e2e.ts`, `client-today.e2e.ts` and `me.e2e.ts` extended, and a new `train.e2e.ts`. Live, on a scratch demo that gains a house (the demo generator's part of this row): a person signs up, confirms and lands on Today; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, no sideways scroll, 200% text. The first download measured before and after.

**Left out.** Start a workout, the player and the pool (N9b3, N9b4). A link to `/train` from the coaches' homepage (N9's Open: none). The offer's words beyond a few plain lines, which are the owner's and are listed in section 3.

**Open.** Whether search engines may list `/train` (default: no while sign-up is closed, yes once it is open; the other way: never until the owner says). Whether Plausible counts `/train` (default: no, since N1 fixed what is counted; listed in section 3). The line a trainee reads on Training before N9b3 (default: "Your workouts will start here.").

**As built** (12 Nov 2026; what N9b1 and N9b3 build on). `/train` (`features/train/train.ts`) and `/train/sign-up` (`train-sign-up.ts`) are one lazy route with `sessionKnownGuard` and `HOUSE_BRAND`, in the signed-out frame with `forTrainees` (the photo, no coach summary) or embedded in the shell for someone signed in; their reads are `TrainApi` (outside `ApiClient`) and every page state and refusal is in `train.models.ts`, with `houseMembership` picking TB Gym's space by name for "Open Today". A self-directed trainee's Today and Me read `GET /api/client-profile/me` first and never call `/me/coach` (`selfDirected`, and on Today `selfUnknown` when that read fails); Today's nothing-assigned card reads "No workouts yet" over "Your workouts will start here." (`client-today.html`), where N9b3 puts Start a workout. The House page is `/admin/house` (`features/platform-admin/platform-house*`, `PlatformHouseApi`, every setting's words in `houseSettingWords()`, a `Record` over `HouseSetting`, so a later row's setting needs words before it compiles), linked from the four places the other admin pages are. The demo builds "TB Gym coaching space" as the house beside either cast, with `nadim@tbgym.example` (owner) and `joelle@tbgym.example` (coach) and every setting empty, and no trainee (section 3 says why); a live pass fills the settings from the House page and signs up through `/train`.

### N9b1 · Free: the plan that never ends, and the pool's read

**Follows.** Decisions 26 and 27 as the owner changed and checked them on 11 Nov 2026, 30 and 34; MED-002. Builds on N9a1.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9b" (the plan that never ends, Free under a paid plan, the pool, the demos) and from N9a1 as built. Server only. (1) The Free plan: a Free product in the house, an ordinary `CoachingProduct` with a zero-price `ProductOffer` covering Training, which the server starts by itself the day a sign-up is confirmed (idempotent on the profile) and which never ends: default, a long fixed length that a Worker job renews before its last day as a new linked row (SUB-003, never an edit). Default, the command that marks the house makes the Free product and its offer when the house has none, so a house is never without one. (2) Free stays under a paid plan: its offer lets Training be covered beside another plan (`AllowsConcurrentCoverage`, SUB-004's stated exception), the GiST exclusion untouched and still keeping two paid plans apart. Free raises no plan notices. (3) The tier: read on the server from the house's plans that grant today (default: a tier named on the house's product, Free, Pro+ or Max, set by the server for Free and by the house's staff for a paid one; the other way: worked out from the offer's features and price). (4) The pool's read: a new client read lists the house's exercises in use for a house trainee who has Training access, each with its name, type, equipment, movement, muscles, instructions and demos, searched as the coach's library is (LIB-003), never an archived one (LIB-004), never another workspace's. A coach's client still reads no library. (5) The demos' grant: a house trainee may open a pool exercise's media without a program holding it (ADR 0011 and the media grant amended); nobody else gains anything.

**Rules.** N9's. The money objects never collapse: Free is a zero-price enrollment like any other, Active at creation (SUB-008). `B9ExerciseLibraryTests` keeps a coach's client's refusal. A trainee writes nothing to the pool.

**Where.** N9's Where: Money, The exercise pool, Demos, Worker.

**Tests.** Domain: Free's renewal dates (half-open, each row following the last), the tier from the plans that grant today. Integration: Free starts once on confirmation and never twice, the renewal racing a booking, the GiST overlap untouched by Free beside a paid plan and still refusing two paid plans at once, the pool's read (in use only, searched, another workspace's refused, nothing without Training access, a coach's client still refused), the media grant for a pool exercise and for nothing else, no plan notice raised by Free.

**Left out.** A workout (N9b2). The pool's page and the looping clip (N9b4). Any limit (N9b5). An open-ended enrollment, which touches the half-open periods and platform billing and needs a domain decision.

**Open.** The Free offer's length and how early the Worker renews it (default: the chat's, named in section 3; it is plumbing, and a trainee never reads an end date). Whether My plan shows Free at all (default: one line, "Free, with no end date").

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; SUB-019, LIB-006, MED-017; what N9b2 to N9c5 build on). The tier is `HouseTier` (Free, ProPlus, Max) on `subscriptions."CoachingProducts"`, copied onto each `ClientEnrollment` when it is booked; both never change, and a trigger refuses a tier on a plan made outside the house. Free is `HouseFreePlanRule` (365 days, renewed from 30 days before a row ends, the next row from that row's end and linked to it): `platform-admin house set` makes the house's one Free plan (`HouseFreePlan.EnsureAsync`, refused while the space sells a plan of its own called Free), `TraineeSignUpService.JoinAsync` starts it for the confirmation and the signed-in join alike (`StartAsync`, command id `HouseFreePlanRule.StartCommandId(profile)`), and `IHouseFreePlanRenewal`, run on the Worker's hourly house tick beside the sign-up sweep (whose switch, `TraineeSignUps:SweepEnabled`, never stops it), renews each current trainee's last row, one already ended included, and starts Free for a self-directed trainee who has none; `house clear` takes Free off sale and is refused while a plan of a tier is booked. Every staff write to Free answers 409 `house_free_plan`; `POST /api/commercial/products` takes `houseTier` (ProPlus or Max, in the house alone) and the product and enrollment views carry it. `GET /api/client-plan/me` leaves Free out of `plans` and adds `tier` (`HouseTierReader`) and `free` (`name`, `since`, `status`, `features`); no screen reads them yet. Free never stands for the client's plan while another plan or a pack runs (the access decision, the Clients row and the client page's server reads, the ask to renew, a lapsed row included) and never covers a program; the web's own client header and Start a plan's overlap line still read Free as a plan (section 3). The pool is `GET /api/exercise-pool` and `GET /api/exercise-pool/{exerciseId}` (`IExercisePoolService`, TenantClient; 404 outside the house or with no current profile; `isAllowed` and `accessReason` as the Training reads), each item a `PoolExerciseView` whose `demos` (`mediaAssetId`, `kind` Picture, Video or VideoLink) are ready media alone; the media grant opens such a demo for a client of the house with Training access. Ending a relationship takes the profile's row lock before it cancels plans, Free with them.

### N9b2 · Free: a workout of one's own, the server

**Follows.** Decision 26 as the owner changed it on 11 Nov 2026 (Free is the pool, and a trainee logs workouts of their own from it), 28 (a workout of one's own is not a routine) and 32 (no calculations on Free); the default the owner was told on 12 Nov 2026: a workout may be started from a past one. Builds on N9b1.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9b" ("A workout of one's own", "No calculations"), N9's Where ("A workout") and N9b1 as built. Server only: the model, its migration and the client routes; N9b3 is the player. A trainee starts a workout with no program behind it, adds exercises from the pool, logs each set (weight, unit, reps, effort, a note) and finishes; it then stands in their history, counts for their records (TRN-019), feeds "last time" (TRN-029) and can be shared (TRN-030), as a program's workout does. Default, the same workout tables with no session behind the row: the mesocycle and session ids and the prescription ids may be empty together and only together (a CHECK), the exercise is its own "prescribed" one so the swap CHECK stands, exercises and sets are added while the workout is in progress, and history, records, "last time" and the player keep one source; the other way, tables of its own, which leave the protected rows untouched and pay for it with a second copy of every read and of the player. An exercise added by mistake leaves before the workout is finished, without any row of a program's workout becoming deletable. A new workout may be started from one in the trainee's history: its exercises in their order, no weight carried in as prescribed. One unfinished workout of one's own at a time. Today's read for a self-directed trainee says whether one is in progress, and the week's counts take their own workouts in.

**Rules.** N9's "A workout of one's own is the trainee's record". TRN-008 and TRN-012 gain the case and the protections stay: a finished workout frozen, nothing rewritten, no delete, Training access asked on every write (`ClientExecutionRefusalAsync`). Typed weights only: nothing is worked out from a max, an effort chart or "Last time + step". Only a house trainee starts one: a coach's client trains on what their coach gave them. ADR 0007 amended; the new rules numbered in DOMAIN-RULES.

**Where.** N9's Where: A workout, Training, The exercise pool.

**Tests.** Domain: an exercise in use in the house's pool, typed weights only, frozen once finished, the CHECK's two shapes. Integration: started, an exercise added and taken away, sets saved, finished, then read in history, records and "last time"; another trainee's is 404; a coach's client refused; refused without Training access; the workout triggers still refusing a delete or a rewrite of a program's workout; two starts at once; the migration's revert.

**Left out.** The player and the exercise picker (N9b3). Saved routines (28, Step 5). A trainee's own exercises. Any calculation (Pro, Step 5).

**Open.** How many exercises and sets one workout may hold (default: the limits `RecordActual` and the builder already keep). Whether an unfinished workout of one's own closes by itself (default: no, as a program's does not; "Carry on" stays).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; TRN-033, ADR 0007 amended, migration `OwnWorkouts`; what N9b3 builds on). The same workout tables: `WorkoutExecution.MesocycleId` and `TrainingSessionId` and the exercise and set prescription ids are nullable, empty together and only together (`CK_WorkoutExecutions_ProgramOrOwn` and two checks on the child rows; the exercise and set triggers refuse an insert whose shape differs from its parent's), `IsOwn` says which, and `UX_WorkoutExecutions_OneOwnInProgress` keeps one unfinished at a time. The client routes, each answering 403 while Training is closed: `POST /api/training/me/workouts` with `{ fromWorkoutId? }` (the body may be left out), for a current client of the house alone (404 elsewhere), at most ten a house day; then, on a workout of the caller's own (404 for another client's, or for an exercise or set not in it; 409 `own_workout_only` for a program's), `POST /workouts/{id}/exercises` with `{ exerciseId, version }` (an exercise in use in the pool, else 404); `DELETE /workouts/{id}/exercises/{exercisePerformanceId}` and `POST /workouts/{id}/exercises/{exercisePerformanceId}/sets` and `DELETE /workouts/{id}/sets/{setPerformanceId}`, each with `{ version }`; every one answers the whole `OwnWorkoutView` (`workoutExecutionId`, `name` "Workout", `date`, `status`, `version`, `exercises` with `performanceId`, `exerciseId`, `exerciseName`, `position`, `mediaAssetIds` read from the pool, `previousPerformance`, `sets`, and `notes`). Sets are logged with the existing `PUT /workouts/{id}/sets/{setId}` and the workout finished with the existing `POST /workouts/{id}/complete`, whose finish summary carries the records. Refusals by code: `own_workout_in_progress`, `own_workout_daily_limit`, `own_workout_nothing_to_copy`, `own_workout_only`, `own_workout_limit`, `own_workout_not_last_set`, `own_exercise_has_note`, `workout_immutable`, `concurrency_conflict`, and on a note `own_workout_staff_note`. `platform-admin house clear` refuses while the house holds a workout of one's own. `GET /me/today` carries `ownWorkout` (the unfinished one, any day), `GET /me/week`'s days `ownCompleted`, and `GET /me/history`'s items `isOwnWorkout` with `programName` empty. The coach's workout read answers 404 for an own workout and `LiftStandingsReader` never reads one; records, last time, Coach Today's records and the Clients row's last activity count it. `HousePool` (Infrastructure) is the pool's one definition, shared with `ExercisePoolService`.

### N9b3 · Free: a workout of one's own, on screen

**Follows.** N9b2's API; 25's note and the defaults the owner was told on 12 Nov 2026.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9b2 as built. Web only. Today for a self-directed trainee: "Start a workout", or "Carry on" while one is unfinished, where a coach's client reads their session. The player for a workout of one's own: an exercise picker over the pool (search, the exercise's demo), add an exercise, add and log sets (weight, unit, reps, effort, a note) with "last time" beside them, take an exercise away before finishing, finish. It is the player a program's workout uses, with nothing prescribed shown. History lists a workout of one's own beside a program's, and "Start from this workout" opens a new one from it. Records and share cards as for any workout.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md's kit and definition of done; the player's motion stays as built. Nothing is refused only in Angular.

**Where.** `features/training/today-training.*` (the player), `features/dashboard/client-today.html`, the client's history and records under `features/training/`; N9's Where: Training.

**Tests.** Vitest: Today's card in its two states, the picker, add and take away, logging, finish, Start from this workout. Mocked Playwright: `client-today.e2e.ts` and `client-training.e2e.ts` extended. Live: a trainee starts a workout, adds two exercises, logs, finishes and finds it in history; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, no sideways scroll, 200% text. The first download measured before and after.

**Left out.** The pool's browse page and the looping clip (N9b4): the picker shows a demo as the player does today. Saved routines.

**Open.** The picker's order (default: by name, the trainee's most used first once they have history; the other way: by muscle). The words of the empty states.

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; what N9b4 and N9b5 build on). Web only, no rule changed (TRN-033 gained a sentence on its screens). The calls are two services outside `ApiClient`: `features/training/own-workout-api.ts` (`OwnWorkoutApi`: start, from a workout or empty, add and remove an exercise, add and remove a set) and `exercise-pool-api.ts` (`ExercisePoolApi.search`, query, skip and take, 50 a page). `own-workout.models.ts` plays an `OwnWorkoutView` as the player's own workout shape with nothing prescribed (`ownWorkoutAsPlayed`, `isOwnWorkout`; its session key is its id, so `/training/today?sessionId=<workout id>` opens it) and words the refusals (`startRefusalWords`, `changeRefusalWords`). The player (`today-training.*`, `today-training-own.scss`) puts Today's `ownWorkout` first, keeps one finished elsewhere read only, and opens `features/training/pool-picker.*` (`app-pool-picker`: `PagedSearch` over the pool in name order, the coach's `app-exercise-demo` per row at a touch size, Done in the sheet's footer) from "Add an exercise". Today: `trainingCard` answers `own-in-progress` first (Carry on), a trainee's `nothing-assigned` card is the start card (`startCard()`), and the week's days carry `ownCompleted` (`weekDayDone`). Training: `HistoryWorkout.isOwn`, the trainee's empty state and "Start from this workout". Today always reads `GET /api/client-profile/me` (N9a2); the player and Training read it only with no program and nothing to show. N9b4's pool page can reuse `ExercisePoolApi`, the picker's rows and `OwnWorkoutApi.start`.

### N9b4 · Free: the pool's page and animated clips

**Follows.** Decision 26 as changed on 11 Nov 2026 and the owner's word of 12 Nov 2026: the animated exercises are wanted, the 3D library is not bought yet, and the build is made for it. C20's fourth slice, a demo that plays like a GIF. Builds on N9b1 and N9b3.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9b" (the pool, the demos) and from N9b1 and N9b3 as built. The pool's page for a house trainee: browse and search the house's exercises, filter them, open one (its animated demo, instructions, muscles, equipment) and start a workout with it. The animated clip: an exercise's uploaded MP4 or WebM plays like a GIF, muted, looping and inline, in the pool, in the picker and in the workout player, for every client and coach (today a demo opens in an overlay with play controls); a picture stays a picture, and a YouTube or Vimeo link still opens on the provider's page. A clip loads only when it is seen, and a person who asked for reduced motion gets a still with a play control. An exercise with no demo shows none. The pool works with whatever demos the house's Coach has uploaded; N9b6 loads the library when its files exist.

**Rules.** The MED rules and ADRs 0023 to 0025: a clip is served through the existing grant, never a public address. The pool is a lazy route and no clip is in the first download. No GIF upload is added: the library's GIFs are converted to MP4 loops before they are loaded (C20).

**Where.** N9's Where: The exercise pool, Demos; `features/training/exercise-demo.ts`, `external-video.ts`, `today-training.html`.

**Tests.** Vitest: the pool's list, search and filters, an exercise's page, the looping player and its reduced-motion still. Mocked Playwright: a new pool spec, and `client-training.e2e.ts` extended for the player's clip. Live: a trainee browses, opens an exercise, watches the clip loop and starts a workout from it; a coach's client's player shows the looping clip too; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, 200% text; the first download before and after.

**Left out.** The loader (N9b6). A 3D figure the trainee can turn (C20: not worth it now). Instructions in a coach's client's workout, which the coach's exercise sheet promises ("Clients read this in their workout, beside the demo.") and no client read carries: work outside the row, listed in section 3 with a recommendation.

**Open.** The filters (default: muscle and equipment, as the coach's library filters). Whether a clip plays by itself on a phone (default: yes, muted and small; the other way: tap to play).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; MED-018, LIB-006's sentence on screen; what N9b5 and N9b6 build on). Web only, on N9b1's and N9b2's API: no API, table or migration. The clip is `features/training/demo-clip.ts` (`app-demo-clip`: `demos` as `ClipDemo` `{ id, kind }` with the pool's kind or null, `exerciseName`, and `decorative` for a list's thumbnail) over `demo-clips.ts` (`DemoClips`: the grants, the one pause every clip follows, `chosenDemo`, `CLIP_MOMENT` "N9b4" for `tb-motion-off`), with `demo-clips-toggle.ts` (`app-demo-clips-toggle`, "Pause demos" and "Play demos") for a list. A clip asks for nothing until it is near the screen, then for its grant through the batch route (6 a request after one antiforgery read, kept 20 minutes, at most 96 live a visit before it waits for a press, forgotten on a workspace change; a list's thumbnail held back asks again when it next comes near or on "Play demos"); an upload plays muted (the property and the attribute), looping and inline with no controls, stops off the screen, starts as its first frame (`#t=0.001`) for reduced motion, the motion switch or Save-Data, and asks one fresh grant when its bytes are refused; a picture is a picture and a link opens on its site. The pool's page is `/training/pool` (`exercise-pool.*`, `exercise-pool.models.ts`; the address keeps `?q=&muscle=&equipment=`) and an exercise's is `/training/pool/:exerciseId` (`pool-exercise.*`), both lazy behind `clientGuard`; `ExercisePoolApi` gained `muscle` and `equipment` (sent after `query`, only when set) and `get(id)`. The player draws `app-demo-clip.exercise-clip` under the shown exercise's name while it is not swapped (`shownDemos`, styles in `today-training-clip.scss`), the picker a square thumbnail in `.demo-slot` (a link keeps its demo button) with the toggle beside its search, and the player's overlay and the coach's demo sheet play an upload muted and looping with their controls. The kit's chip takes `size="touch"` (44 px, its items too). "Browse exercises" opens the pool from Training's "Train on your own" and Today's start card, for a self-directed trainee alone. First download 518.31 kB before and 518.31 kB after.

### N9b5 · Free's limit, Progress and the house's storage

**Follows.** Decision 27 as the owners ticked it (a window on Free's charts) and as checked against Hevy's own pages on 11 Nov 2026; the default the owner was told on 12 Nov 2026: no workout is ever locked. Decision 26. Builds on N9b3.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9b" ("The limit", Progress, the storage allowance) and from the rows before it as built. Server and web. (1) The limit: a house setting, empty by default, caps how far back a Free trainee's Progress reaches; every logged workout stays readable in history whatever its age; what is over the limit is kept and opens again under any paid plan, and Progress says so in one sentence. The server applies it from the tier (N9b1), never Angular alone. (2) Progress for a trainee with no program: its training part counts finished workouts, where it counts sessions done against sessions planned for a coach's client. (3) The house's storage: a per-workspace override of `MaxWorkspaceStorageBytes` that the platform admin sets, so the house has room for the clips. (4) The House page gains both settings.

**Rules.** The numbers are the owner's, entered in the app, never a constant, a seed or a doc. An empty window means no limit. A paid plan lifts the limit for as long as it grants.

**Where.** N9's Where: Progress, Media; N9a2's House page.

**Tests.** Domain: the window against the tier and the day. Integration: Progress inside and outside the window on Free, whole under a paid plan and cut again once that plan ends, history always whole, another trainee's refused, the override honoured for the house and for no other workspace, an upload refused over it. Vitest and mocked Playwright for Progress's sentence and the House page; a live pass with stills.

**Left out.** A limit on saved routines (28: routines are Step 5). Locking older workouts (the other way, N9's Open). Every number.

**Open.** The sentence's words (default: one plain sentence naming the window from the setting and saying everything logged is kept; listed in section 3). Whether the personal-records list is cut by the window too (default: no, records are history).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; PRG-015, MED-019, CLI-023 and TRN-033 amended, ADR 0041 and ADR 0014 a paragraph each; what N9b6 and N9c2 build on). No table or migration: two more `HouseSetting` values, `FreeProgressDays` (whole days, 1 to 3,650; empty, no limit) and `MediaStorageGigabytes` (whole binary gigabytes, 1 to 10,240; empty, the platform's allowance), each on `PUT /api/platform-admin/house/settings/{setting}` and in `GET /api/platform-admin/house`, the House page showing "30 days" and "50 GB". The reach is `FreeProgressReachRule` ("FreeProgressReach" 1.0, Subscriptions): `[today - N + 1, today + 1)` while the house's window is filled, `HouseTierReader` reads Free and no plan of the house without a tier, priced above nothing, is active and paid in full today; it is worked out in `ProgressApplicationService.Reach.cs` for the client's own reads alone (`/api/progress/me/dashboard`, `/me`, `/me/span`, `/me/measurements`, `/me/photos`), each of which moves its window inside it with `ProgressReachWindow.Clamp` (never a refusal), reads nothing older (a trend's warm-up, a whole week, an earlier day) and carries `reach` (`methodKey`, `methodVersion`, `windowDays`, `earliestShown`); the staff's reads, by-id reads, history and records stay whole. `WorkspaceCalendar.IsHouse` makes the reach cost nothing outside the house. The dashboard's training context gains `ownWorkoutsCounted` (the house alone), `recentOwnCompletedWorkoutCount`, `windowOwnCompletedWorkoutCount` and `lastOwnCompletedDate`, by the house day each workout of one's own started. The media upload reads its allowance once (`HouseReader.MediaStorageBytesAsync`, the house's while it is the active house and the setting is filled) for the pre-check and the locked check alike. On screen, Progress and Your entries say "On Free, Progress shows your last {n} days. Everything you log is kept, and all of it shows here again on a paid plan." while a reach applies, their empty words name the window, and Training reads the workouts finished where nothing is planned. First download 518.31 kB before and after.

### N9b6 · The demo library's loader

**Follows.** The owner's word of 12 Nov 2026: the animated exercises are wanted and nothing is bought yet, so the build is made for them. C20: a one-time library whose licence allows a paid app and keeping and converting the files, each GIF converted to an MP4 loop. Builds on N9b4.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9b" ("The 3D motion library") and from the library and media code as built. A command beside `platform-admin` that loads a library into the house's exercise library alone: a folder of clips (MP4 or WebM) and a sheet (CSV, UTF-8) with one line for each exercise: name, type, equipment, movement, muscles, instructions and the clip's file name. It makes each exercise with its clip through the services the coach's screens use, so every validation, the scan and the storage allowance apply; it is safe to run again (an exercise is matched by its name in the house, and its clip replaced only when the file changed); it has a dry run that prints what it would do; and it reports each line it refused with the reason. The sheet's form is TB Gym's own, written down beside the command, because no library is bought yet: when the owner buys one, fitting its file list to the sheet and converting its GIFs to MP4 loops is a small step done then, with the real files in hand.

**Rules.** Into the house alone (LIB-001: under the library's licence, into no other workspace): the command refuses any other workspace. No library file, name list or clip is committed to this repository, which is public; the tests use a few clips the test makes itself. Nothing here reads a licence or decides a purchase.

**Where.** `TB.Gym.Api/Program.cs` (the `platform-admin` command); N9's Where: The exercise pool, Demos, Media.

**Tests.** Integration: a small folder and sheet loaded, run again with nothing doubled, a changed clip replaced, a bad line reported and the rest loaded, another workspace refused, the allowance honoured, the dry run changing nothing.

**Left out.** Buying the library and reading its licence (the owner's, C20). Converting its files (done when they exist; a converter would be a new dependency, named and explained then). Picking the clips (the trainer partner's).

**Open.** Whether the loader also archives a house exercise the sheet no longer names (default: no, it only adds and updates).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; LIB-007, ADR 0041 a sentence; what a bought library is fitted to). Server only: no table, migration, route or screen. The command is `house-library <the house's slug or id> <sheet.csv> [--dry-run]` (`Initialization/HouseLibraryCommand.cs`, wired in `Program.cs` beside `platform-admin`): the named space must be the house and active, the clips are named from the sheet's folder (sub-folders allowed, never outside it), and it acts as the house's Coach who joined first, else the Owner, through `DemoRequests` (a fresh scope per call). The sheet's form is `ExerciseSheet` in the ExerciseLibrary module (a CSV reader of its own, strict UTF-8 with or without a byte-order mark, quoted line breaks kept, the first line naming name, type, equipment, movement, muscles, instructions and clip in any order; `ExerciseSheetWords` reads each word as the API spells it or the screens say it, never a number or a list; the first muscle is the main one; a line refused with every reason it has, a second line for the same name refused), written down in the two classes' remarks and in GO-LIVE's "The house's library". Each line: the clip checked in place (inside the folder, MP4 or WebM, `MediaUploadPolicy.Validate` on its first bytes, its SHA-256), the house's exercise of that name read with its demos, the library's own rules run in memory (`Exercise.Create`), an archived exercise refused, then a new exercise made with its clip (`CreateAsync`) or an existing one updated (`UpdateAsync`: the sheet's type, equipment, movement, muscles and instructions, an empty instructions cell keeping the house's; its name, tags, swaps and other ready demos kept); a clip whose bytes a demo already has changes nothing, else the loader's own earlier clip of the same file name (titled "<name> (library clip)") is replaced in its place and tombstoned through `DeleteAsync` unless another exercise shows it, else the clip goes first (refused at ten demos); a change to an exercise with an archived swap is refused before any upload; a clip uploaded for a save the library then refuses is deleted again. A full allowance, uploads off, nobody to load as or a running upload stops the run ("Not reached: n", what to wait for). Output: "The house stores X of its Y allowance." (`MediaApplicationService.StoredBytesAsync`), one line per sheet line (added, updated with what changed, unchanged, or refused with the reason), the counts and the clips uploaded; the dry run says "would be added/updated" and whether the uploads would fit. Exit 0 all loaded or unchanged, 1 a line refused or the run stopped, 2 before line one.

### N9e · Closing and anonymising an account

**Follows.** The plan's first-version list (ready before the first stranger joins) and the launch checklist's privacy item. Builds on N9a1; placed here so that Free is whole before the paid plans are built.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9e" and from the code as built. Server and Me. A trainee closes their account from Me, after a confirmation that says what is erased and what is kept: closing ends every membership, anonymises the person (name, email, phone, the health fields, intake answers; photos tombstoned and purged through the media purge, ADR 0014) and keeps the facts that may not be deleted under the anonymous profile: payments, enrollments, finished workouts and records (SYS-004). It is a defined operation, never a row delete: the append-only triggers stay as they are. The platform admin can do the same on a request by hand.

**Rules.** N9's. Nothing sensitive in a log line. A closed account can never sign in again; its email may sign up again as a new person. A staff account, an owner's or a coach's, is not closed here.

**Where.** N9's Where: Clients (`/api/client-profile/me/leave`, ADR 0027), Media (the purge, ADR 0014), Legal; `features/profile/` and Me.

**Tests.** Domain: anonymising keeps the facts. Integration: closing ends every membership; the anonymous profile keeps payments, enrollments, workouts and records; photos are purged; the triggers still refuse a delete; the closed account cannot sign in; another person's account cannot be closed; the admin's route refused to anyone else; no health field in a log line. Vitest and mocked Playwright for Me's Close account; a live pass with stills.

**Left out.** How long each kind is kept (the lawyer's list; the retention periods are settings, left out until the owner has them). Closing a staff account. An export of one's data before closing.

**Open.** Whether a coach's client gets the same button (default: yes, on every client's Me, since the operation is the same and the checklist's privacy item is for everyone; the other way: house trainees only). What the client's coach is told (default: the notice a leaving client raises today, and nothing more).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; CLI-027, MED-020, ADR 0027, 0002 and 0014 amended; what a later privacy or retention row builds on). Identity owns the routes, the record and the port; `AccountClosureService` in Infrastructure composes the closing. `POST /api/auth/account/close` with `{ password }` (signed in, no workspace header read, antiforgery, the sensitive-write limit) answers 204 and signs out; `POST /api/platform-admin/accounts/close` with `{ email, requestedOn }` (from 1 Jan 2026 to today anywhere) answers 200 `AccountClosureView` (the day, the spaces left, the records anonymised, the photos removed). Refusals: 400 `password`, `email`, `requestedOn`; 404 `account_not_found`; 409 `account_closure_staff` (an Owner or Coach membership, current or ended, anywhere), `account_closure_platform_admin`, `account_closure_changed` (a concurrent save, all rolled back), `account_already_closed`. One transaction: a waiting sign-up's lock and the account's first, then a scope a space joining the account's connection; each current record is left through `ClientProfileApplicationService.EndInsideTransactionAsync` (the leave's own steps, factored out of `EndRelationshipAsync`), every record of the person anonymised (`ClientProfile.Anonymise`, `AnonymisedAtUtc`, change source `AccountClosure`), accepted invitations anonymised (`ClientInvitation.Anonymise`), declared allergens deleted, photos removed and their assets due for the purge at once (`MediaAsset.PurgeForClosedAccount`), a waiting sign-up deleted, the account emptied and locked; one append-only `identity."AccountClosures"` row. Migration `AccountClosure`: the table, `CK_ClientProfiles_Anonymised`, `CK_ClientInvitations_Anonymised`, the intake check relaxed for an anonymised record, the freeze and invitation triggers letting the anonymising through once, and `identity.refuse_closed_account` refusing at commit any new active membership, client record or upload for a closed account. On screen: Me's `app-close-account` under Sign out (`features/account/close-account.*`, `CloseAccountApi`, `closeAccountRefusal`), the same as a panel on Password and security for someone with no coaching space, and sign-in's `?reason=account-closed` sentence (`ACCOUNT_CLOSED_REASON`). First download 518.31 kB before, 518.35 kB after.

### N9c1 · Asking for a plan and paying, the server

**Follows.** Decisions 29, 30, 34 and 36; the owner's word of 11 Nov 2026 (a plan is booked when its money is recorded) and of 12 Nov 2026 (built now and kept switched off); 3 and 13: no trainee pays before the company's Whish account exists. Builds on N9b1.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9c" ("The plans", "Asking and paying") and from the commercial code as built. Server only. The house's paid plans are ordinary products and immutable offers the house's staff make in Plans you sell, each naming its tier (Pro+ or Max, N9b1). "Ask for a plan" stores an append-only ask naming the offer, on the model of `RenewalRequest`, and books nothing; "I've paid" adds the Whish name and number used; each tells the house's staff in-app, and they are the only staff inbox notices a self-directed trainee raises. When the money is there, one staff route books the plan from that workspace day and records its receipt in one transaction, idempotent on its key, the exact price only, the same currency (SUB-007, SUB-008). The next pass is asked for the same way and booked from the day after the running one ends, or from the day its money is recorded when that is later. One paid plan at a time, with Free running underneath. Switched off (12 Nov 2026): an ask is refused, and the trainee's read offers none, while the house's Whish number is empty or the offer is not on sale; the number joins the house's settings.

**Rules.** N9's money rules. A paid plan is booked when its money is recorded, never when it is asked for. A part payment books nothing and grants nothing: the exact price only, the rule coaching spaces already keep; booking on a part would be a new money rule and is left out. No FX, credit, refund, waiver or recurring billing. The GiST exclusion and its concurrency test stay intact. An idempotency key is bound to its payload.

**Where.** N9's Where: Money, Notices; `RenewalRequests.cs` as the model for an ask.

**Tests.** Integration: an ask books nothing and leaves Free granting; "I've paid" on one's own ask only; one step books and records, twice with one key returns the first result, and a changed payload with that key conflicts; a wrong amount or currency refused; two asks at once; one ask booked twice; a coach's offer cannot be asked for by a trainee, nor the house's by someone who is not a house trainee; an ask refused while the Whish number is empty; the append-only trigger on asks; each staff notice raised once.

**Left out.** The screens (N9c2). The programs and Start (N9c3). Max's places (N9c5). The cancellation window (the lawyer's wording). Card payments and Whish automation (Step 5).

**Open.** The house's Whish number as data the platform admin enters (default) or a configuration key beside `PlatformBilling:WhishNumber`, which needs a deployment to change. Whether an ask ever lapses (default: no; the house's staff close one with a reason, which is kept).

**As built** (13 Nov 2026; SUB-020, CLI-023, CLI-026, CLI-027, SUB-014, NOT-006, ADR 0041 amended; what N9c2, N9c3 and N9c5 build on). The house's Whish number is the setting `WhishNumber` (5): text, single spaces, an optional +, 6 to 20 digits, 40 characters at most; empty keeps asks closed. The Subscriptions module owns `HousePlanAsk` (the ask: trainee, plan, option, tier, the house's day), `HousePlanTransfer` (the trainee's word that the money was sent, with the Whish name and number, once an ask) and `HousePlanAskOutcome` (Booked with the plan, its receipt and the staff's key, or Closed with a reason), all append-only (`subscriptions."HousePlanAsks"`, `"HousePlanTransfers"`, `"HousePlanAskOutcomes"`), and `HousePlanAskRule` (what may be asked for: a Pro+ option on sale priced above nothing, Max closed until N9c5; the Whish details' form; `BookingStart`). `HousePlanAskService` in Infrastructure composes them, each write under the trainee's profile row lock. The trainee: `GET /api/house-plans/me` (`HousePlansView`: today, `asksOpen`, `whishNumber`, `plans` with name, words, price, currency, length and features, `asks` newest first), `POST /api/house-plans/me/asks` `{ offerId }` (201; the same option again answers the open ask; 409 `house_plan_asks_closed`, `house_plan_not_on_sale`, `house_plan_ask_open` naming the plan and option asked for, `client_blocked`), `POST /api/house-plans/me/asks/{askId}/money-sent` `{ whishName, whishNumber }` (409 `house_plan_money_already_sent`, `house_plan_ask_ended`); 404 outside the house or for anyone not a current self-directed trainee. The staff (Owner, and a Coach for their own trainees): `GET /api/commercial/clients/{clientProfileId}/house-plan-asks`, `POST .../{askId}/book` `{ amount, currencyCode, reference, idempotencyKey }` (one transaction: the plan active from the house's day or from the end of the last plan it cannot run beside, the trainee's other Pro+ or Max or a plan holding one of its features alone, a mobile-wallet receipt of the exact price, the outcome; 400 `amount` for a part or a wrong figure, `currencyCode`; 409 `house_plan_ask_ended`, `idempotency_key_reused`, `client_blocked`, `entitlement_overlap`; the same request again answers the first booking) and `POST .../{askId}/close` `{ reason }`. Each `HousePlanAskView` carries the plan's name, option, price and length, the state (Asked, MoneySent, Booked, Closed), the word on the money and the outcome (with the plan's first and last days; who decided for the staff alone). Notices `HousePlanAsked` and `HousePlanMoneySent` (workspace kinds 15 and 16, in-app, to the Owner and the trainee's coach, linking the client's page). The booked plan raises no plan notice; a self-directed trainee never asks to renew the coaches' way. The database: `EX_ClientEnrollments_OneHousePaidPlanAtATime` (a trainee's Pro+ and Max rows not cancelled never overlap), triggers keeping asks in the house, by self-directed trainees, for an option of the plan and tier named, a booking naming the trainee's own plan of that option and a receipt of its whole price, and the word on the money changing only by the closing's erasure; migration `HousePlanAsks`. The account closing erases the Whish details. On screen only a guard: the House page leaves off a setting it has no words for yet (the Whish number's line is N9c2's). First download 518.35 kB before and after. The web API client was not regenerated: N9c2 does it with the screens.

### N9c2 · Asking for a plan and paying, on screen

**Follows.** N9c1's API.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9c1 as built. Web only. The trainee's plans page, which is My plan for a house trainee: Free's line, each plan the house has on sale with its name, price, length and the words its staff typed, "Ask for" that plan, then the house's Whish number and "I've paid" with the Whish name and number used, and where the ask stands. The staff's side: the two inbox notices, and a booking sheet that books the plan and records the receipt in one step from the ask. The House page gains the Whish number. While asks are switched off the page shows Free alone.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md. Prices and plan words come from the house's offers; none is typed in Angular.

**Where.** `features/account/client-plan.ts`, `features/commercial/`, the staff inbox; N9a2's House page.

**Tests.** Vitest and mocked Playwright (`client-service.e2e.ts` and `me.e2e.ts` extended): the page switched off and on, the ask, I've paid and each state after; the staff's sheet. Live, on a scratch demo's house: a trainee asks for a plan and says they paid, the staff book it, and the trainee's page shows it; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, 200% text; the first download before and after.

**Left out.** The programs page (N9c4). Max's places line (N9c5).

**Open.** The words of each state (defaults, listed in section 3).

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; web only, on N9c1's API; what N9c4 and N9c5 build on). The web API client was regenerated from N9c1's server. The trainee: My plan (`features/account/client-plan.*`) reads `GET /api/house-plans/me` through `HousePlansApi` (`house-plans-api.ts`, outside the eagerly loaded `ApiClient`) only when `/api/client-plan/me` carries `free`, so a coach's client never calls it; a 404 shows nothing of the house, any other failure a line of its own with Try again. `house-plans.models.ts` holds the pure mappers (the plans on sale grouped by plan in the server's order, the length left out where the option's name already is it, the pills, the open ask, the earlier asks, `whishNumberValue` and `whishNumberProblems` mirroring the server's rule, `houseRefusal`); `house-plan-ask-sheet.ts` (the confirm, its words a focusable region) and `house-plan-paid-sheet.ts` (I've paid) post with antiforgery, cannot be closed while their answer is on its way, and have the page read the house again after a refusal that means it is out of date, focus kept on the page. The staff: the client's Service page (`features/clients/client-service.*`) reads `GET .../house-plan-asks` through `HousePlanAsksApi` only for a client holding a Free plan (`houseTier` now on the web's `ClientEnrollment`); the panel's mappers are `house-plan-asks.models.ts`, the booking sheet `house-plan-book.ts` (`IntentIdempotencyKey`, the amount prefilled with the exact price) and the closing dialog `house-plan-close.ts`, both reporting busy and stale to the page, which rereads the plans and the asks afterwards; `serviceWriteError` shows the server's words for `house_plan_ask_ended`. The notices: `noticeTarget` opens `/clients/{id}/service` for `HousePlanAsked` and `HousePlanMoneySent`; `undelivered-notices.models.ts` names both. The House page: `HouseSettingWords` is a union of a number kind and a text kind; the Whish number is shown as stored, left to right, and its sheet is a text box checked by `whishNumberForm` as the server checks it; `emptiedWords` has no default, so a later setting needs its own words. First download 518.35 kB before and after; My plan's styles compile to 7.49 kB, past a screen's 5 kB warning and under its 8 kB error.

### N9c3 · Pro+: the programs and Start, the server

**Follows.** Decision 29 (the trainer partner's programs one at a time, access for the paid period only), 30 (the trainer partner's rule says whom each program suits) and the fix of 11 Nov 2026 (a long program is written as blocks). Builds on N9c1.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9c" ("Pro+, the programs", "A program longer than a pass") and from the training code as built. Server only. The house's Coach marks which published programs trainees may start, each with a line on whom it suits and its place in a run of blocks; a client read lists them for every house trainee, with a sample week. Start is a new client route: it copies a published program as the trainee's own snapshot with every week showing (TRN-006), inside the pass's dates through `TrainingCoveragePolicy` (TRN-011), one at a time, with no "program assigned" notice (NOT-035). A block fits a pass only if it ends inside it: the read says the last day a block can still be started, and Start is refused after it with the reason. The next block opens once the one before is finished or its pass has ended, under the next pass, the trainee's maxes and history carried on. The trainee types their own maxes before the first week when the program works its weights out from them: a client route beside the coach's, checked for sensible values and append-only (STR-015). With Free underneath Training is always granted, so a program's sessions open only while the plan the program was started under still grants. A self-directed trainee may start any session of the current week: TRN-020's `session_not_due` is relaxed for them to the workspace week.

**Rules.** N9's. One program at a time: the primary-program overlap constraint stays. Starting a program makes a snapshot, and the house's later edits never change a started program. No silent rebase after a max changes, and kg and lb are never converted implicitly. When a pass ends the trainee is on Free, and what they logged stays theirs and readable.

**Where.** N9's Where: Training, Strength, Money.

**Tests.** Domain: one program at a time, a block that would end past its pass refused, the last day to start, the next block's opening. Integration: Start under a pass and refused on Free, a second Start refused, a session refused once the pass no longer grants while Free still grants Training, a session of this week on another day, the trainee's max route (their own profile only, append-only, a coach's client refused), another trainee's program 404, two Starts at once.

**Left out.** The programs page (N9c4). A pass as long as the program, or a program running across passes (N9's Open: a domain decision). Own routines and "turn this routine into a block" (Step 5).

**Open.** How the house's Coach orders blocks (default: a run named on the program, with its place in the run). Whether a trainee may start a later block first (default: no).

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; TRN-034 and STR-021, TRN-011, TRN-020 and SUB-019 amended, ADR 0041 and ARCHITECTURE; what N9c4 builds on). Server only; the web API client was not regenerated (N9c4 does it with the screens). The Training module owns `HouseProgramListing` (the staff's entry for a published program: whom it suits, an optional run name and place, the rounding a trainee's weights take, offered or withdrawn, under a row version), `HouseProgramStart` (append-only: a trainee's start, with the run and place then, the version and the Pro+ plan) and `HouseProgramRules` (HouseProgramStart v1: the terms, the unit, the fit and last start day, a run's opening, the current program week, the max pick), in `training."HouseProgramListings"` and `"HouseProgramStarts"`, kept in the house by triggers, a start checked by `training.guard_house_program_start()` to name the trainee's own program of that version under their own Pro+ plan; migration `HousePrograms`, whose revert stops once a start exists. The staff (the house's Owner or Coach; 404 elsewhere; blocked while the house's invoice is unpaid): `GET /api/training/house-programs` (`HouseProgramListingView`: template name, latest published version, weeks, unit or `MixesUnits`, suits, run, rounding, offered, times started, version), `PUT /api/training/templates/{templateId}/house-listing` `{ suits, runName?, runPosition?, loadIncrement?, loadRoundingMode?, version? }` (400 `suits`, `runName`, `runPosition`, `loadIncrement`, `templateId`; 409 `house_program_run_position_taken`, `concurrency_conflict`) and `POST .../house-listing/withdraw` `{ version }`. The trainee (a current self-directed trainee of the house; 404 for anyone else): `GET /api/training/me/house-programs` (`HouseProgramsView`: today, `pass` (the Pro+ plan that grants today, or null), `running` (the program holding today or later), `programs` each with name, description, suits, run, weeks, unit, `sampleWeek` (week 1: sessions, lifts, set counts, reps), `maxes` (each lift a max feeds, with the value it would start from and `from` Typed or LastProgram), `canStart`, `refusal` (Start's code, or `training_closed`), `opensOn`, `lastStartDay`, `timesStarted`) and `POST /api/training/me/house-programs/{listingId}/start` `{ templateVersionId, idempotencyKey }` (200 `HouseProgramStartedView`; 403 Training closed; 409 `house_program_not_offered`, `house_program_needs_pass`, `house_program_changed`, `house_program_unit`, `house_program_block_not_open`, `mesocycle_overlap`, `house_program_does_not_fit`, `house_program_needs_maxes`, `idempotency_conflict`), one transaction under the trainee's row lock (`/* house-program-start */`) through Assign program's snapshot step (`AddMesocycleSnapshotAsync`), every week published, RevealAllWeeks on, no notice; it first closes a block whose Pro+ pass was cancelled. `POST /api/strength/me/maxes` `{ exerciseId, value, unit }` records a trainee's training max (method `TraineeTyped` 1.0; 400 `value`; 404; 403; 409 `house_program_needs_pass`). In the house a program's sessions follow its own plan (`HouseProgramPlans`): start, set saves, swaps and the client's notes answer 409 `program_plan_closed` once it stops granting, a started workout may still be finished, and Today, Upcoming, the week and Today's coverage leave a begun closed program out. A self-directed trainee starts a session of the program's current week early: the program read's `ClientProgramSessionView.CanStartEarly`, Today lists an early session while open or on the day it was done, and last time counts it from that day.

**Follows.** N9c3's API.

Today and Training then read the started program as a coach's client's do. The coach's side: marking a published program as one trainees may start, with its line and its place in the run.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md.

**Tests.** Vitest and mocked Playwright (`client-training.e2e.ts` extended). Live: a trainee under a pass starts a block with their own max and trains a session, and the page shows when the next block opens; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, 200% text; the first download before and after.

**Left out.** Max (N9c5).

**Open.** The words, listed in section 3.

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; web only, on N9c3's API; nothing for N9c5 to wait on). The web API client was regenerated from N9c3's server. The trainee: `/training/house-programs` (`features/training/house-programs.*`, under the tab bar with Training lit) reads `GET /api/training/me/house-programs` through `HouseProgramsApi` (`house-programs-api.ts`, outside the eagerly loaded `ApiClient`); a 404 shows "Programs aren't open here", a 403 or `training_closed` the Training-closed words. `house-programs.models.ts` holds the pure parts: the pass card, a card's block, weeks, sample week and maxes lines, `refusalWords` (N9c3's sentences from a refusal's code, `opensOn`, `lastStartDay`, the pass and the running program, since the read carries no sentence), `runningStranded`, `startQuestion`, `startRefusal` (a Start refused for a cause the read did not know reads the list again) and the max check (`TRAINEE_MAX_LIMIT`, two decimals). On Free the page reads N9c2's `HousePlansApi` (`tier` now on `HouseOffer`) to say whether Pro+ can be asked for, and links to My plan. Start asks first, then posts with an `IntentIdempotencyKey` bound to the listing and version, and opens Training; `house-program-maxes-sheet.ts` posts `POST /api/strength/me/maxes` once for each changed lift in the program's unit. Training (`training-program.*`) now reads the profile whether or not a program runs, links a self-directed trainee to the page, offers their own workouts once no program runs (`ownWorkouts`), maps `canStartEarly` to "Start now", and for a trainee's running program reads Upcoming: when it has no program, `planClosed` shows N9c3's closed-plan sentence and leaves every session row plain. `api-error.ts` words `program_plan_closed`; the player (`today-training.ts`) shows it on a refused set, swap or note without reading the workout again. The coach: `HouseListings` (`house-listings.*`, `house-listing-sheet.ts`, `house-listings.models.ts`, `HouseListingsApi`) sits on the Programs page (`program-list.*`, now guarded by `confirmSheetLeave`); `HouseListingsMemo` remembers, per workspace and session, that the list answered 404, which is how the web knows a space is not the house. The coach's max history words `TraineeTyped` as "Typed by the trainee". The mocked specs are `e2e/house-programs.e2e.ts` (new), `training-program.e2e.ts` and `program-builder.e2e.ts`: `client-training.e2e.ts`, which the Tests line names, is the coach's view of a client and was not changed. First download 518.35 kB before and after.

### N9d1a · The owner privacy split, the server

Split from N9d1 on 14 Nov 2026 by its chat: the access split and the client with no coach were two chats' work (the second touches about 25 files and a migration of its own), and the split's may coach already reads an empty coach as nobody, so the two can follow one another. N9d1b is the second part.

**Follows.** C8's decision of 14 Oct 2026 (docs/CONCERNS-BRIEF.md) and decision 33: before Max opens. It changes every coaching space, not the house alone.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from C8, N9's part "N9d" and `CoachClientScope` as it stands. Server only. `CoachClientScope` answers two questions, may manage and may coach, and every client route is sorted into one: the Owner sees the business side of every client (name, coach, plan and payment status, renewals, activity counts), and the private side (training and food logs, check-in answers, weigh-ins, measurements, progress photos, intake answers, the health fields) opens only to the client's own coach. An owner who coaches is simply the coach of their own clients. Notifications, search, share cards and exports follow the same sorting. The client with no coach is N9d1b's.

**Rules.** ADR 0026 is amended (a business decision already taken), and CLI-013 with it. A route that reaches a client still answers 404 for a client the caller may not see in that way. A released client stays read-only (ADR 0027).

**Where.** `Infrastructure/Application/CoachClientScope.cs`, `Infrastructure/Security/TenantAuthorization.cs`, every module's client routes; N9's Where: Tenancy and scope, Clients.

**Tests.** Integration: the owner refused on another coach's client's private routes and allowed on the business ones, route by route; another workspace's owner. The architecture tests if a contract moves.

**Left out.** A client with no coach (N9d1b). The screens (N9d2). The front-desk role and its two choices (C8's slice 2).

**Open.** Which side a read C8 does not name falls on (default: private; each one listed in section 3).

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; CLI-013, CLI-015, CLI-018, CLI-020, CHK-014, NUT-008, PRG-007, SES-008, ADR 0026, 0027 and 0011 amended; what N9d1b and N9d2 build on). SharedKernel's `ClientSide` (`Business`, `Private`) sits on the route groups (Training's, Nutrition's, Strength's, Progress's, both Check-ins' client groups and Clients' are private; `/api/commercial` and the house's plan asks are business) and on seven Clients routes (`GetClientForCoach`, `GetClientOverview`, block, unblock, reassign, the coach history, release); `TenantRoleAuthorizationHandler` reads it only on a route naming `clientProfileId` or `clientId` and asks `CoachClientScope.ExcludesFromManagingAsync` (business) or `ExcludesFromCoachingAsync` (private, and any route with no marker), both answering 404 `client_not_assigned` before the released 409 and the read-only 403. `CoachClientScope.MayCoachAsync(staffUserId, clientProfileId)` is the private side's one answer (assigned to them, active Owner or Coach, and for a released record no `Released` or `ClientLeft` history entry) and `StaffUserIdAsync` filters the private lists; the old `ExcludesAsync` became `ExcludesFromManagingAsync`. Indirect rows: programs by id and their training maxes, workout notes (the Owner too now) and lift weights may coach; progress-photo media asks `MayCoachAsync`; enrollments, payments and sessions may manage; messaging is unchanged (`IsAssignedCoachAsync`). Mixed reads: `CoachClientDetails.MayCoach` (the 14 private fields null when false, on every route returning it) and `CoachClientRef.MayCoach` (Today, the Clients rows, a client's page: no goals, birth date, last activity, last workout, missed sessions' day or check-in to open; Week not shared, No program and Weights to apply read only for the clients the caller coaches; the feed leaves the others out). `GET /api/checkins/to-review` and `GET /api/nutrition/client-targets` list the clients the caller coaches. No migration; the web API client is not regenerated. Tests: `N9d1aOwnerPrivacySplitTests.cs` (the route sort pinned, route by route both sides, the writes, the indirect rows, the owner's own clients, taking and handing back a client, Today and the lists, missed sessions, former clients, another workspace), and the tests that had the Owner read another coach's client's private side moved to the client's coach.

### N9d1b · Waiting for a coach, the server

**Follows.** C8's decision of 14 Oct 2026 (a departing coach's clients wait for a coach; an owner who coaches never gets them automatically and takes one by assigning it to themselves, and the client is told), on N9d1a as built. Split from N9d1 on 14 Nov 2026.

**Builds.** Server only.
