A client's coach may be empty: ADR 0026's "exactly one coach" becomes "at most one" (CLI-012), `ClientProfile.AssignedCoachUserId` and the coach history's coach become nullable, and the database guards change with them in one migration (the chain check and its trigger read a waiting entry, the client-coach trigger accepts an empty coach only with a matching newest entry; the departing-staff, append-only and invitation guards stay). Removing a coach or a coach resigning leaves their clients and their pending client invitations waiting, with a waiting entry in the history and nothing deleted; their programs and meal plans keep running and they can still log. An invitation accepted after its coach left lands waiting. The owner takes a waiting client through the reassignment route, as any coach is assigned, and every coach change, an owner taking a client included, tells the client in-app (a new notice kind; until it exists a reassignment tells nobody, so an owner may take a client to read its private side with only the history knowing); every coach change stays in the append-only history. The reads follow: the two business lists that join the coach today keep a waiting client (the Clients list, Coach Today's scope), while the check-ins to review and the client targets, private since N9d1a, list only the clients the caller coaches and so leave a waiting client out; the Team page's counts skip the empty coach, Today gives the owner the clients waiting for a coach, and This month counts a waiting stretch for nobody with a line that makes the owner's rows add up. The words of the coach-departed notice and the owner's resignation notice change, since nobody else looks after the client now.

**Rules.** ADR 0026 amended again and CLI-012, CLI-014, CLI-024, SUB-016 and SUB-018 with it. Money attribution keeps its fixed reference tests. A released client stays with the owner as keeper (ADR 0027, N9d1a). N9d1a's may coach already treats an empty coach as nobody: nobody opens a waiting client's private side.

**Where.** `Modules/Clients/ClientProfile.cs`, `ClientCoachAssignment.cs`, `Infrastructure/Application/TeamApplicationService.cs`, `CoachTeam.cs`, `InvitationApplicationService.cs`, `ClientProfileApplicationService.cs`, `CoachOverviewReader.cs`, `CoachMonthReader.cs`, `ClientRenewalService.cs`, `HousePlanAskService.cs`, the notices, a migration; the N9d1 readers' map in this row's chat (14 Nov 2026) lists about 25 files.

**Tests.** Integration: a coach leaving leaves their clients and invitations waiting with nothing deleted; an acceptance after the coach left; a waiting client assigned and told; the history through a waiting stretch; the database guards (a NULL without its entry, a chain through NULL); money during a waiting stretch; another workspace's owner; the Team page with a waiting client. Domain: the profile, the history entry, This month's rows.

**Left out.** The screens (N9d2). The front-desk role (C8's slice 2).

**Open.** Whether a house trainee with no house Coach waits or keeps the Owner (default: waits, as C8 says the owner never gets clients automatically); who an Ask to renew tells while the client waits (default: the owner, renewals being the business side); whether clients the owner holds today from an earlier coach's departure are turned into waiting ones (default: no, they stay the owner's).

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; CLI-012, CLI-014, CLI-015, CLI-020, CLI-024, CLI-026, SUB-016, SUB-018 v3, NOT-006, ADR 0026, 0027 and 0041, ARCHITECTURE, GO-LIVE and the README amended; what N9d2 builds on). `ClientProfile.AssignedCoachUserId` and `ClientCoachAssignment.CoachUserId` are nullable (`IsWaitingForCoach`, `WaitForCoach()`; `ForDeparture` writes a departure's entry naming nobody, `ForChange` always names a coach and may follow a wait; no new reason: taking a waiting client is `Reassigned` from nobody); `ClientInvitation.WaitForCoach()` replaced `HandOverTo`; `CoachTeam.ResolveStartingCoachAsync` gives nobody instead of the owner, for an invitation whose coach left and for the house with no Coach. Migration `20261114081122_WaitingForACoach`: both columns nullable, the chain CHECK rewritten with total terms, the chain trigger reading nobody and refusing a new departure entry that names anybody, the client-coach trigger accepting an empty coach only with a matching newest entry, `CK_ClientProfiles_ReleasedHasKeeper`, and the outbox kinds `CoachAssigned` (17) and `TraineeStaffChanged` (18); its Down refuses (55000) while anything waited. The reassignment queues `CoachAssigned` ("You have a new coach") to a coached client and `TraineeStaffChanged` ("Who can see your training changed") to a self-directed trainee, in-app only, keyed by the history entry, suppressed once a later change follows or the client is released; CoachDeparted and CoachResigned are at template version 3. Reads: `ClientSummary`, `CoachClientDetails` (coach null, name "" while waiting), `CoachClientRef` and `ClientCoachAssignmentView` carry a nullable coach; Clients, Today and the overviews left-join; `CoachTodayView.WaitingForCoach` (`CoachWaitingClientView`: the client, `WaitingSinceUtc`, `PreviousCoachName`, `ClientVersion`) is the Owner's alone, never a self-directed trainee, one tagged read only when somebody waits; the Team members' counts leave a waiting client out; `CoachMonthReportView.NoCoach` (`CoachMonthNoCoachView`) is the Owner's alone, null when nothing counted for nobody, from `MonthNumbersPolicy.CountForNobody`, and `MoneyReceivedPolicy.CoachAt` is null in a wait; a waiting client's Ask to renew stores and tells the owner; the house's plan asks and a leave tell the owner alone; `/api/client-profile/me/coach` answers 404 while waiting; to review and the client targets return nothing without a caller. No web API client regenerated. For N9d2, on the web: `waitingForCoach` on Coach Today, the `noCoach` line on This month (`features/month/month.models.ts`), the two new kinds in the inbox (`notification-inbox.models.ts` noticeTarget and noticeIcon, `undelivered-notices.models.ts` noticeAbout and noticeAudience), a null coach on Clients (an empty option in the coach chip) and on a client's header ("Coached by"), the coach panel (shown only with more than one member; "The current coach loses access"), the Team page's remove words and toast ("move to you": `team.ts` 288-312, `team.html` 24, `team.models.ts` 60), the coach's Leave page (`leave-workspace.html` 11 and 51), the sessions' blank holder option, and the live spec `e2e/live/team-reassign.e2e.ts`, whose resign and remove tests still expect the owner. Tests: `N9d1bWaitingForACoachTests.cs` (19: a removal and a resignation leaving clients and invitations waiting with nothing deleted, an acceptance after the coach left, the owner taking a waiting client and every change told, the history through a wait, the database guards, money in a wait with the rows adding up, another workspace, the Team page, a house trainee with no Coach, a waiting client's Ask to renew, a leave and a release from a wait, the migration's way back cause by cause, the waiting read's query count) and `N9d1bWaitingDomainTests.cs` (16), with fixed reference cases added beside SUB-016's and SUB-018's, and the tests that had a departing coach's clients move to the owner, or the house's Owner read a trainee's training, moved to waiting or to the house's Coach.

### N9d2 · The owner privacy split on screen

**Follows.** N9d1a's and N9d1b's API; C8.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9d1 as built. Web only. Coach Today lists "clients waiting for a coach" first for an owner. An owner who opens another coach's client sees the business side and a plain line on why the rest is the coach's. A waiting client is assigned, to oneself or to a coach. The Team page's sentence ("you see every client") changes to say what an owner sees. The client is told of a coach change as today.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md. Angular's role checks are presentation: the server refuses (N9d1).

**Where.** `features/dashboard/coach-today.*`, `features/clients/`, the Team page.

**Tests.** Vitest and mocked Playwright (`coach-today.e2e.ts` and the clients and team specs extended). Live, on the demo: the owner's view of a coach's client, a coach leaving, a waiting client assigned; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, 200% text; the first download before and after.

**Left out.** The front desk (C8's slice 2).

**Open.** The words, listed in section 3.

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; web only, on N9d1a's and N9d1b's API; nothing for N9c5 to wait on). The web API client was regenerated (the nullable coach of `ClientSummary`, `CoachClientDetails`, `CoachClientRef` and `ClientCoachAssignmentView`, `mayCoach`, `waitingForCoach`, `noCoach`, the two notice kinds); the owned `CoachClientDetails.mayCoach` reads a missing value as true. Today (`coach-today.*`): an owner's `waitingForCoach` is a panel of its own first, "Waiting for a coach" with its count, each row "Since <day> · Coached by <name> before" or "No coach yet" and "Choose a coach", a link to `/clients/{id}/service?action=coach`; the greeting says how many wait; an item about a client the caller does not coach (`mayCoach` false) keeps its sentence, names who follows up and opens the client's page with "See client" (`attentionTarget`, shared with the Clients tags); a waiting client's other rows say "· Waiting for a coach"; one filled button (`primaryAction`: the first waiting row's Choose a coach, else the first row the caller can act on); the This month card gains a No coach line; the statuses sheet says who follows up another coach's client's items. A client's page (`client-workspace.*`, `client-overview.*`): `context.mayCoach`, `waitingForCoach` and `closedSide()` leave Overview and Service & access; the private children of `clients.routes.ts` carry `side: 'private'` and the workspace draws the why panel in place of the outlet, so no private component or request is made (`confirmPageLeave` for Edit weeks and the check-in review); the Overview reads only the overview for such a client and opens with the why panel (another coach's client, a waiting client, a former client the caller did not coach); the header says "Waiting for a coach" and offers a waiting client no Message. The coach panel (`client-coach.*`) shows for a waiting client even to an owner alone (`canChange`, and it stays drawn with its notice after a save), says "Choose a coach" with nothing chosen and You last, says what the choice does and "<name> is told of the change.", and opens itself on `?action=coach`; history entries naming nobody read "Waiting for a coach" and "after waiting for a coach". Service & access's session holders leave an empty coach out. Clients (`clients.*`): a waiting row says so, the Coach chip gains "Waiting for a coach" (`?coach=waiting`), a row the caller does not coach says nothing about activity. Team, Leave and This month (`features/team`, `leave-workspace.*`, `features/month`): clients wait for a coach and Clients lists them, the owner is told, This month's No coach row and sentence. Notices: CoachAssigned and TraineeStaffChanged open Today with the clients icon and have words on the undelivered list. The client's own screens: a coached client whose `/me/coach` answers 404 reads "Waiting for a coach" on Today and "Waiting for a new coach" on Me, with no Message your coach; the intake, check-in and photo privacy lines say "you and your coach". The live spec `e2e/live/team-reassign.e2e.ts` follows the waiting behaviour and has the owner take a waiting client from Today. First download 518.35 kB before, 518.37 kB after.

### N9c5a · Max, the server

Split from N9c5 on 14 Nov 2026 by its chat: like N9c1 and N9c2, N9c3 and N9c4, N9d1 and N9d2, the server and the screens are two chats' work. N9c5b, the screens, follows on this part's API. What follows is N9c5's outline as written; this part builds its server side.

**Follows.** Decisions 30, 31, 33 and 36, and the owner's word of 11 Nov 2026: Max is TB Gym coaching the trainee, the four things a coach's client gets, for a limited number of places. After N9d1 and N9d2, as the tiers' table says.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9's part "N9c" ("Max, by hand", "Max's places") and from the rows before it as built. Server and web (N9c5a the server, N9c5b the screens). (1) Max's places: a house setting the owner fills from the trainer partner's hours, kept by the server; empty means Max is closed (12 Nov 2026: built switched off). While every place is taken an ask for Max is still stored, in order, and the page says a place is not open yet. (2) A Max trainee is coached, not self-directed, for as long as Max grants: Coach Today, check-ins and Messages as for a coach's client, and self-directed again when it ends. (3) The Health step opens only on Max: the intake's five steps skip 'health' for a house trainee who is not on Max (CLI-004 still keeps birth date, height and a goal). (4) The rest of Max exists and is used by hand: the house's Coach assigns the picked program through the builder, builds and assigns the meal plan from the house's recipes with its calculation of the trainee's targets, assigns a check-in form and answers in Messages.

**Rules.** N9's health rule: health answers only in the app, in no log, notice or analytics, and no AI reads them (36). The Owner sees a Max trainee's business side only (N9d1). Max's offer covers Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging; what a month of Max holds is the partners' words, typed on the offer, and the screen says only what they wrote.

**Where.** N9's Where: Intake, Nutrition, Clients (`CoachAttentionPolicy`), Money.

**Tests.** Domain: Max's places. Integration: the last place asked for twice, an ask stored in order when every place is taken, Max closed while the setting is empty, the Health step refused off Max and open on it, a Max trainee on Coach Today and off it once Max ends, the Owner refused on a Max trainee's health answers. Vitest, mocked Playwright and a live pass for the places line, the intake and Today.

**Left out.** What a month of Max promises (the partners'). The later paid consultation, more coaches and call-takers (30). One-to-one sessions (18). The lawyer's question on a calorie-counted meal plan (31).

**Open.** Whether a place is held from the ask or from the booking (default: from the booking, in the asks' order, so an unpaid ask holds no place).

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; SUB-021 and CLI-028 new; CLI-014, CLI-018, CLI-020, CLI-023, CLI-026 and SUB-020 amended, ADR 0041 and ARCHITECTURE; what N9c5b builds on). Server only, no migration; the web API client was not regenerated (N9c5b does it with the screens). Tenancy's `HouseSetting.MaxPlaces` (6): a whole number from 0 to 1,000, empty or 0 keeping Max closed (`HouseSettings.MaxPlaces`). Subscriptions' `MaxPlaces.cs`: `MaxPlacesRule` ("MaxPlaces" 1.0: a place is held by a Max plan not cancelled and still to end on the house's day, or by an open Max ask whose money its trainee said was sent, which they may only while its place is open; the free places; the line of the other open Max asks in the order asked, ordered in SQL by time asked then id, a trainee who left dropped; `MayBook`, `WouldHaveAPlace`) and `MaxPlanRule` (each Max option covers Training, Nutrition, CheckIns and Messaging, all four: 400 on `product` when a plan is made, on `offer` when an option is added). `HousePlanAskRule.IsAskable(product, offer, maxOpen)`; `HousePlanAskService` lists and takes Max asks while Max is open (closed: `house_plan_not_on_sale`), and the trainee's read gains `PlaceOpen` on each Max option (`HousePlanOfferView`) and each open Max ask (`HousePlanAskView`), the staff's `PlaceInLine` as well (null on the trainee's); "money sent" on a Max ask and a Max booking take a house-wide advisory lock (`/* house-max-places */`) after the trainee's row lock, and answer 409 `house_max_closed`, `house_max_place_not_open` (money sent) or `house_max_full` (a booking, after its replay); `CommercialApplicationService`'s Start a plan and Renew answer 409 `house_max_by_ask` for a Max offer. Coached while Max grants: `HouseTierRule.CoachesTheTrainee`, `HouseTierReader.TiersAsync` (one command tagged `TiersTag` for a whole scope, none outside the house or with no trainee in it) and `CoachedOnMaxAsync`; `CoachOverviewReader`'s `ScopedClient.OwnCoachToday` feeds the attention signals, the Owner's waiting list, the activity feed and the list row, with no plan end or asked renewal for any house trainee (attention stays v9); `TeamApplicationService.DepartAsync` tells a Max trainee `CoachDeparted`, a Max trainee's workout note reaches their coach, a reassignment sends `CoachAssigned`, and leaving or closing the account tells the Owner and their coach (`ClientLeft`). The Health step: `ClientProfile.UpdateIntake` and `CompleteOnboarding` take `healthStepOpen` and refuse an added or changed health answer with `HealthStepClosedException` (one kept or emptied passes), answered 409 `health_step_closed` (`ClientCommandStatus.HealthStepClosed`) on the trainee's and the staff's intake routes and on complete-onboarding. Reads: `ClientSelfProfile.CoachedByTheHouse` and `HealthStepOpen` on `GET /api/client-profile/me` and on every answer that returns the own profile; `CoachClientDetails.HealthStepOpen` on the staff's reads. For N9c5b: the four `house_max_*` codes and `health_step_closed` need words; the House page leaves Max places out until it has words (`houseSettingWords`, `emptiedWords`); `planChoices` in `client-program-forms.models.ts` still offers Free, so Assign program's first choice is refused for a Max trainee. Tests: `N9c5MaxPlacesTests` (9), `N9c5MaxCoachedTests` (5) and `N9c5HealthStepTests` (6) on the shared `N9c5MaxTestSupport`, domain `N9c5MaxPlacesDomainTests` (13), `N9c5MaxCoachedDomainTests` (3) and `N9c5HealthStepDomainTests` (11); N9a1's settings pins and trainee intake, and N9c1's and N9b1's Max plans (now of four features), adjusted.

### N9c5b · Max on screen

**Follows.** N9c5a's API (its As built above), split from N9c5 on 14 Nov 2026.

**Builds.** An outline; its chat writes the spec from N9c5a as built. Web only. The web API client is regenerated. My plan for a house trainee: a Max option and a Max ask say when a place is not open yet, and a Max ask whose place is not open shows neither the Whish number nor "I've paid"; the refusals `house_max_place_not_open` and `house_max_closed` read in words and have the page read the house again. The House page gains Max places (a number sheet; empty or 0 keeps Max closed), named so it is not read as the week's sign-up places. The staff: the asks panel on a trainee's Service & access shows a Max ask's turn and whether its place is open, and the booking sheet words `house_max_full` and `house_max_closed`; Start a plan and Renew word `house_max_by_ask`; Plans you sell words a Max plan's four features. The intake and My profile leave the Health step and card out while `healthStepOpen` is false (four steps), keep answers already saved readable, never send them emptied, and word `health_step_closed`; the staff's intake for such a trainee does the same from the client's `healthStepOpen`. A trainee with `coachedByTheHouse` reads Today's and Me's coach card as a coach's client does (the coach, Message your coach), the house's own screens (the pool, workouts of their own, the programs page) staying theirs; the programs page says a Max trainee's program comes from their coach rather than offering Ask for Pro+. The house Coach's Assign program leaves Free out of its plan choices, so a program is assigned under Max.

**Rules.** UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md. Prices, plan words and what a month of Max holds come from the house's offers; none is typed in Angular. Angular's checks are presentation: the server refuses (N9c5a).

**Where.** `features/account/client-plan.*` and `house-plans.models.ts`, `features/platform-admin/platform-house*`, `features/clients/house-plan-asks.models.ts` and the booking sheet, `features/commercial/`, `features/intake/`, `features/profile/`, `features/dashboard/client-today.*`, `features/account/client-account.*`, `features/training/house-programs.*`, `features/clients/client-program*` and `program-assign.ts`.

**Tests.** Vitest and mocked Playwright (`me.e2e.ts`, `client-service.e2e.ts`, `intake.e2e.ts`, `client-today.e2e.ts`, `platform-house.e2e.ts`, `house-programs.e2e.ts`) for the places line, the intake and Today. Live, on a scratch demo's house: Max's places filled, two trainees ask with one place, the first pays and is booked, the second reads that a place is not open; the Max trainee's Today shows their coach and Messages, their intake has the Health step and a Free trainee's has four; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, 200% text; the first download before and after.

**Left out.** What a month of Max promises (the partners'). The later paid consultation, more coaches and call-takers (30). One-to-one sessions (18).

**Open.** The words, listed in section 3.

**As built** (14 Nov 2026; web only, on N9c5a's API; no rule changed, so DOMAIN-RULES, the ADRs and ARCHITECTURE stay as N9c5a left them). The web API client was regenerated from N9c5a's server (`placeOpen`, `placeInLine`, `coachedByTheHouse`, `healthStepOpen`, `MaxPlaces`); the owned `ClientIntakeProfile.healthStepOpen` reads a missing value as open, `ClientSelfProfile.coachedByTheHouse` a missing one as false, and `CoachingProduct.houseTier` is typed. My plan (`house-plans.models.ts`, `client-plan.*`, `house-plan-ask-sheet.ts`): `HouseOffer.placeOpen`, `HouseAsk.tier` and `placeOpen`; a plan's `placeLine` when its options read `placeOpen` false, tied to its Ask buttons by `aria-describedby`; the ask sheet's waiting words (`data-ask-waits`); an Asked Max ask with `placeOpen` false (`waitingForPlace`) reads "Waiting for a place" and N9c5a's sentence and draws no Whish number, Copy, "If you already sent the money" or I've paid; with its place open "A place on Max is open for you now." comes first; every `house_max_…` code is stale (the server's words, then the house read again, focus on the card's status line), and a reread clears the earlier news. The House page (`platform-house.models.ts`, `platform-house.ts`): `MaxPlaces` as "Trainees on Max at a time", 0 to 1,000, and its toasts. Me (`client-account.*`): the coach read skipped only for a trainee who is their own coach; "Your goals and contact info" while the Health step is closed. The staff (`house-plan-asks.models.ts`, `client-service.*`, `house-plan-book.ts`, `client-service-forms.models.ts`, `client-service.models.ts`, `client-overview.models.ts`): `placeOpen` and `placeInLine` on the staff's ask, the four turn lines (`data-place`), the pill, the booking sheet's warning (`data-no-place`), `asksOutOfDate` for `house_max_full` and `house_max_closed`, `house_max_by_ask` worded, `PlanCard.tier`, no filled Renew and a disabled Manage plan Renew on a Max plan, `?renew=` opening no sheet for Max, and `renewalPrompt` never offering Max. Plans you sell (`products.models.ts`, `product-sheets.ts`): `MAX_FEATURES`; Add price option on a Max plan has the four ticked and locked and shows a refusal under What's included. Assign program (`client-program-forms.models.ts`, `program-assign.ts`, `client-program.ts`): `planChoices` without Free, `onFreeAlone` and its words. The intake (`intake.models.ts`, `intake-steps.*`, `intake-profile.*`, `intake-edit.ts`, `client-profile.ts`, `client-intake.ts`, `client-workspace.ts`): `healthOpen`, `intakeSteps`, `intakeGroups`, `withSavedHealth` (while closed every save sends the saved health answers as they are), `isHealthStepClosed`, `stepShown` and `stepAfter` (a step gone mid-session gives way to the next), a progress grid of as many columns as steps, the closed Health card (`data-health-closed`), the 409's words and the reread, focus kept on a card heading when the Health card goes under an open sheet, and Edit intake's hint. Today (`client-today.*`): `houseTrainee` beside `selfDirected`, which now means a trainee who is their own coach today, and `coachedByTheHouse`; the start card's `data-max-coach` line and one `data-max-message` link. The programs page (`house-programs.*`): the profile read beside the plans when no pass runs, `coachedOnMax`, the Max branch. Messages needed no change: the tab follows the Messaging feature, which Max grants. First download 518.37 kB before, 518.53 kB after.

## 3. Waiting for the owner

Choices the rows made on their own, each with the other way. Nothing here blocks a row.

- **N1, the sign-up page's count.** Decision 23 (still to explain) says what the counts include; until it is picked, N1 counts the homepage and What changed for a visitor alone, and the sign-up page's one count is left for N2's chat. The other way: count it in N1 now. Recommendation: wait; it is one line either way. Answered on 7 Nov 2026: yes, count it; N2 adds the count.
- **N1, where the two counted addresses live.** The homepage (`/`) and What changed (`/changelog`) are one constant, `COUNTED_PAGES` in `core/website/website-analytics.ts`, which the browser's guard reads; the homepage counts itself at `/` alone, so a second address for it (N2's `/about`) counts nothing. The other way: list them beside the routes. Recommendation: keep; a website page added to the count is one word there.
- **N1, a section link on the homepage.** Following The week, What's inside or For gyms (an address ending in `#…`) counts nothing more, since the visitor stays on the one page. The other way: a count per section, which Plausible's `hashBasedRouting` would give. Recommendation: keep it off; the sections are one page.
- **N1, What changed for someone signed in.** It reads no website settings at all, so nothing of analytics loads there, and after a full page load it decides only once the session check has answered. The other way: read the settings and only never count. Recommendation: keep; the page inside the app needs nothing from them.
- **N1, Plausible's other events.** A new site at Plausible tracks outbound links, file downloads and forms sent by default; the browser turns those three off and its guard drops any event but a page view of the two pages, so a tap on the WhatsApp link or a Talk-to-us form sent is never counted, whatever is ticked at Plausible, and none can fire from a page of the app. The other way: let those switches work on the two pages. Recommendation: keep until you want a form sent counted; it is then one line, like decision 23's sign-up count.
- **N1, a tab opened behind and a return from the browser's cache.** As Plausible's own counting did, a page opened in a background tab or prepared ahead by the browser counts when it is first shown (not at all if it is closed unseen), and a page the browser brings back from its back/forward cache (Back after leaving the site in the same tab) counts again. The other way: count such a return nothing, since it runs no app code; the catch of counting it is that someone who signed in from another tab meanwhile is counted as the visitor this tab still shows. Recommendation: keep; it is what Plausible counts on any other site.
- **N1, what a count carries.** A count sends the page's path and the campaign tags Plausible reads (`utm_source`, `utm_medium`, `utm_campaign`, `utm_content`, `utm_term`, `ref`, `source`) and no other part of its address, fragment included, so a link tagged `?utm_source=instagram` still shows where visitors came from while nothing else in an address reaches Plausible. The other way: send the whole address, as Plausible's own script does. Recommendation: keep.
- **N1, outside the row: the Talk-to-us form before the page is live.** On a slow connection a gym can send the form before the homepage's code has started; the browser then sends it the old way and puts the name, gym, contact and message in the homepage's address (`/?name=…`), which stays in the browser's history and in nginx's log. N1 keeps it out of the count; the form itself is not changed here. Recommendation: the sign-in page's remedy (Send waits until the page is live, the fields carry no `name`, and `check-prerender.mjs` pins both) as a small row of its own.
- **N1, outside the row: What changed's Skip to content.** Its link (`href="#changelog-content"`, no handler) resolves against the page's base address, so pressing it loads the homepage instead of moving to the releases, and that visit is counted; the app's shell and the legal pages use a `skipToContent` handler for exactly this. Not changed here. Recommendation: the same handler, a one-line fix with a test.
- **N3a, the badge's thresholds.** Decision 15 fixed "at least 10 clients with an active plan" and "after the trial month and at least one more month" but not whether the wait is two or three months, so both thresholds are platform settings with the defaults 10 and 2, changeable by the platform admin without a build. The other way: 3 months. Recommendation: keep 2 until the two of you say 3.
- **N3a, who holds the badge.** The space's active owner, the person who signed and pays: their clients see it, and only the owner reads it and hides it. The other way: the admin names the member who holds it. Recommendation: keep while a founding space is one coach; look again for a gym with several.
- **N3a, whose clients count.** Every client of the space with an active plan right now, counted as the Billing page counts clients with a plan (paid or free, a pack's too, not paused), at this instant rather than over the month, so the Billing page's month-so-far figure can read higher. The other way: only the owner's own clients, or Coach Today's active clients. Recommendation: keep; it is the number TB Gym is paid on.
- **N3a, when the badge is earned.** The check writes it, on the Worker's sweep every 15 minutes and on the admin's Check now, dated the first UTC day it finds both terms met; a read never writes it, so a space that has just met them shows as waiting until the next check. The other way: write it on the owner's or the admin's read. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3a, the months.** Whole calendar months from the UTC day the space was created (31 January plus one month is the last day of February), where the trial starts. The other way: from the founding day. Recommendation: keep; the decision speaks of staying past the trial month.
- **N3a, the end reason.** Free text up to 500 characters, kept for TB Gym's admin alone; N3b offers the three causes (misrepresented qualifications, harm to clients, the coach asks) as presets. The other way: a fixed list of three. Recommendation: keep; a preset still lands as its words.
- **N3a, the hide switch's home.** On the founding status, written by the owner, so it ends with the status and a space marked again starts shown. The other way: on the account, like theme and weight unit. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3a, Settings' signal for N3b.** `foundingCoach` on `GET /api/billing/access`: Waiting or Earned (hidden or not) for the owner of a founding space, null for a coach, an ended status or none; the coach app already reads it once. The other way: a read of its own from the Settings page. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3a, what the owner reads after an end.** The state Ended with the day it ended and the day it was earned, never the reason; N3b shows nothing by default (its own Open item). The other way: read as not founding at all. Recommendation: keep; N3b's other way ("TB Gym ended your founding status on <day>") then needs no server change.
- **N3a, hiding before the badge is earned.** The owner's switch works while the status waits too, and answers 404 when the space has no open status. The other way: only once earned. Recommendation: keep; N3b shows the switch only once earned.
- **N3a, the check on the test copy.** The badge check rides the billing sweep's switch (`PlatformBilling:SweepEnabled`), which the Render test copy turns off with the invoices, so there only the admin's Check now records a badge; GO-LIVE's test copy paragraph says so. The other way: a switch of its own, so the copy checks on its own too. Recommendation: keep; N3b's "TB Gym checks this on its own" holds wherever invoices go out.
- **N3a, outside the row: one more "10 coaches".** LAUNCH-CHECKLIST's privacy item still says "a request from one of 10 coaches is handled by hand"; the row changed nothing else there, as the plan said. Recommendation: "one of the founding coaches" the next time the checklist is read.
- **N3b, the panel's place.** The Founding space panel sits right after "Where it stands", before Invoices, on the space's page. The other way: beside Discounts. Recommendation: keep; it is a standing fact about the space, read before its money.
- **N3b, the terms panel's place.** The Founding coach badge's terms are a panel of their own under Price plan on the list, read apart from billing so a failure there never hides what is owed. The other way: inside the Price plan panel. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, where Settings' row comes from.** The Settings frame reads `GET /api/billing/access` itself, once per Settings page and for the owner alone, and starts from the last word it has in memory for the same space and session, so the row does not blink in on every page; the Founding coach page's own reads are that word too, so a status TB Gym ended takes the row away the moment the page learns it. The other way: a store shared with the coach shell's billing banner, which would land in the first download. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, Today's "Your coach" card.** No badge there: the plan's "under Your coach" is Me's card. The other way: the same pill on Today. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, the pill's tone.** The kit's success tone, for the "Founding coach" pill and the list's "Founding" pill. In dark mode its fill sits close to a coach's brand green on Me's card, so the pill's shape is faint there while its words stay at AA. The other way: neutral. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, the row's place.** Founding coach is the last row of Your account, after Password and security. The other way: first. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, what an ended holder reads.** Nothing: the row goes and the page says "Nothing here" with a way back to Settings; a switch pressed after TB Gym ended the status reads the badge again and lands there too. The other way: "TB Gym ended your founding status on <day>.", which needs no server change. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, the page's guard.** `/account/founding` has the same guard as the other account pages (signed in), as the row says; the page asks the server only for the space's owner and shows anyone else "Nothing here", with a way back to their Settings, or to Me for a client. The other way: the owner guard Billing has, which sends anyone else to Today. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, "your clients".** The owner's waiting sentence says "10 of your clients", as the row words it, while the server counts every client of the space with an active plan, a team coach's too (N3a's choice). The other way: "10 of your space's clients". Recommendation: the other way once a founding space has team coaches.
- **N3b, the switch while waiting.** The hide switch shows only once the badge is earned, as N3a's line said, though the server takes it while waiting too. The other way: show it while waiting. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, Check now's answer.** The toast says what the check found for the space on screen (earned, or not yet), from the page read again; when the page cannot read the space again it says only "The check ran, but this space couldn’t be read again. Read it again to see its badge." The server's counts cover every founding space and are not shown. The other way: show them. Recommendation: keep.
- **N3b, the mark sheet's line.** Under the space's name: "The space’s owner holds the badge once it is earned. Marking changes no access and no invoice." The other way: the name alone. Recommendation: keep; it says N3a's holder where the admin marks.
- **N3b, the causes' words.** The end sheet's presets read "Misrepresented qualifications", "Harm to clients" and "The coach asked to end it" and land in the box as those words, which the admin may change. The other way: other words. Recommendation: keep until the lawyer reads them.
- **N3b, the days.** Every day reads as the app's billing days do ("Oct 3, 2026" in the English build), not the row's "3 Mar 2027". Recommendation: keep; one formatter for every billing day.
- **N3b, Me while the coach is read.** The waiting card keeps the plain card's height, so a founding card, one line taller, moves the groups below once when the read lands. The other way: a taller waiting card for everyone. Recommendation: keep; most coaches hold no badge.
- **N4a, the certificate line's wording.** The line reads "<what was shown>, shown to TB Gym on <date>", one constant in the code, because the lawyer may reword it (Point 2's step 1). The other way: wait for the lawyer before building. Recommendation: build now; the words change in one place.
- **N4a, where the record lives.** In the Identity module, as `identity."CoachCertificateLines"` beside the legal records: a record about a person, kept by TB Gym. The other way: a module of its own. Recommendation: keep; it is one table and one admin service.
- **N4a, the record's tenant.** Each line is tenant-owned (the space where the coach is staff and where the line will show), with the global query filter and the write guard every such row has; the admin's list reads every space past the filter and each write sets its space first, as the billing admin does. The other way: a global record like a legal acceptance, every read naming the space by hand. Recommendation: keep; N4b's reads inside a workspace then cannot see another space's lines.
- **N4a, one line per coach and space.** A coach who coaches in two spaces has their lines recorded in each, and the admin's list shows one row per membership with its counts. The other way: one line per coach, shown in every space they coach in, and the list grouped per person with their spaces under them. Recommendation: keep; a client's read stays exact.
- **N4a, the erasure's record.** An erased line keeps its own row with the words, the day and the confirmer's name blanked and the dates, the reason, the request's day and who acted kept; a waiting line is deleted outright. The other way: a second table of erasures and a hard delete of the lines. Recommendation: keep; one table and one trigger.
- **N4a, the coach's switch.** It is on from the start, since the coach sent the proof to be shown; nothing reads it until N4b. The other way: off until the coach turns it on. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, "PT" and "dietitian".** Neither is refused: a certificate's own name may hold "PT", and the plan names only the physio titles. The other way: refuse both. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, the protected titles' other forms.** "physiotherapists", "physical therapists" and "physios" are refused with the titles, since a plural is the same title, and so is "physical-therapist" with a dash; "physiotherapy", a degree's name, passes. The other way: the three singular words alone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, "certified" in a line.** The server refuses the four words the row names and the titles, so a certificate's own name such as "Certified Strength and Conditioning Specialist" passes, though section 1 keeps "certified" off every screen; the page's own words never hold it. The other way: refuse "certified" too, and write such a certificate as "CSCS". Recommendation: ask the lawyer with the wording; until then keep, since the sentence says the proof was shown, not checked.
- **N4a, words in Arabic.** The refused words are English only: a line typed in Arabic is not checked for an Arabic "licensed" or "approved". The other way: an Arabic list beside the English one. Recommendation: the trainer partner or the lawyer gives the Arabic words, one edit in `CoachCertificateLine`.
- **N4a, who recorded and who confirmed.** Who recorded a line is the admin's account, shown by its display name; who confirmed it is a typed name in a one-box sheet, prefilled with the signed-in admin's own and changeable, so the tech partner can record a "done" the trainer partner sent on WhatsApp. The other way: a typed recorder too, or the confirmer always the signed-in account with no box. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, who took a line down.** The take-down keeps the admin who pressed it beside its time, for the history; the row listed only the time. The other way: the time alone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, erase and its version.** Erase on request takes no version: the row's rules ask for one, but its route takes only the day, and a coach's request covers every line of theirs in the space, those added after the page was read too. The other way: refuse an erasure when a line changed meanwhile. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, the erase sheet's day.** It asks the day the coach's request arrived (the space's today by default, no later) and shows "Requests are met within 10 days: by <day>."; the day is kept on every erased line. The other way: today, without asking. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, the sentence's place and language.** The server writes the finished sentence, with the date in the space's language ("3 Nov 2026" in English, the Arabic month name in an Arabic space), and the page shows it as sent. The other way: the page builds it from the parts. Recommendation: keep; the lawyer's words then change in one place.
- **N4a, the days on the page.** Every day the Coaches page writes (the pills, who acted, the deadline) reads "3 Nov 2026", the form of the sentence beside it, and is the space's own calendar day; the kit's date fields keep their own "Nov 3, 2026", as N3b's billing days do. The other way: the billing days' form throughout. Recommendation: keep; a line and its pill read one way.
- **N4a, a coach who left.** Their membership stays listed while it holds a line, as "Former coach · <space>", with no Add (the server refuses a line for anyone not active staff); their lines can still be taken down or erased, and a waiting one confirmed or dropped. The other way: hide them once they leave, or refuse a confirmation for them. Recommendation: keep; a departed coach's request must still be met.
- **N4a, the same line again after a take-down.** The one-line-per-words-and-day rule covers a taken-down line too, so adding its words and day again answers the taken-down line ("That line is already on the record."); a new day makes a new line. The other way: a taken-down line no longer counts. Recommendation: keep; a proof taken down for cause should not come back unnoticed.
- **N4a, the earliest day.** "Shown on" and the request's day are refused before 1 January 2000, as N3a's founding day is, so a missing date is never stored as year 1. The other way: no floor. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4a, the plain admin bar.** Coaches is also in the top bar an admin with no workspace sees, beside Platform billing and Legal documents, besides the three places the row names. The other way: the three places alone. Recommendation: keep; the three admin pages are found the same way.
- **N4b, the keeping period.** The lawyer sets how long a certificate record is kept; until then `CoachCertificates:KeepMonthsAfterTakenDown` holds the plan's example, 12 months, counted from each line's own take-down (see "per line or per record" below); since nothing deletes an account yet, the sweep looks at taken-down lines alone. The other way: no default, nothing kept until the lawyer answers. Recommendation: the example, since a record without a keeping period would be the worse breach.
- **N4b, the coach's page's place.** Certificates is in Your account right after Password and security, for every owner and coach; a founding owner's Founding coach stays last, after it. The other way: a row in the account menu. Recommendation: keep; it sits beside the person's other account pages.
- **N4b, waiting lines.** A line TB Gym recorded but has not confirmed shows on the coach's page as its sentence with "Waiting for TB Gym" and no switch, so the coach knows what was written; their clients never see it. The other way: hidden until confirmed. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, the switch while the space is read-only.** It saves even while the space's bill is unpaid, as a person's own setting (`SetOwnCoachCertificateLineShown` joins ADR 0028's allow-list): consent can always be withdrawn. The other way: blocked like other writes. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, every shown line on Me.** The client's card lists every line the coach shows, with no fold, since a coach sends few. The other way: fold after three. Recommendation: keep until a coach shows more than three.
- **N4b, the sweep.** A Worker timer erases what is past the period once a day, and once each time the Worker starts; a wrong period or interval stops the API and the Worker at start, naming the setting. No button. The other way: a button on the admin's Coaches page. Recommendation: keep; the law's point is that it happens without anyone remembering.
- **N4b, a coach's "off".** Switching a line off hides it at once and starts no keeping period: the line stays, since the coach can switch it back on; only a take-down starts the period. The other way: an off line counts as removed after the period. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, per line or per record.** Each taken-down line is erased 12 months after its own take-down. The other way: the whole record 12 months after its last line went (the plan's "after the last line expires"). Recommendation: keep; it is at least as protective.
- **N4b, no label over the lines.** The sentences sit under the coach's name in quiet text, with no label. The other way: under a small "Shown to TB Gym" label, a second place for the lawyer's words. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, the switch is a checkbox.** "Show to my clients" is the kit's checkbox, as N3b's badge switch and Notifications' quiet hours are: the kit's Switch (UI plan section 3) was never built. The other way: build the Switch, a `role="switch"` control, and move all three to it. Recommendation: keep until the kit gains a Switch.
- **N4b, the page's kicker.** The page draws the kicker "Your account" above its heading, as the row asks, though no other Settings page has one. The other way: drop it, like its neighbours. Recommendation: keep while it is your word; it is one line to drop if Settings should read alike.
- **N4b, an erased line on the coach's page.** It is not listed: its words are gone, and the admin's page keeps its dates. The other way: "Erased 3 Nov 2026" with no sentence. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, the order.** The coach's page and the client's card list the lines in the order TB Gym recorded them, oldest first; the admin's sheet keeps newest first. The other way: newest first everywhere. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, the admin's pill and the confirmed day.** "Confirmed 3 Nov 2026" became "Shown to clients" or "Hidden by the coach", as the row says, so the day a line was confirmed no longer shows on the Coaches page (the record and the API keep it). The other way: add it to the line under the sentence, "Confirmed by <name> on 3 Nov 2026". Recommendation: add the day there if the trainer partner misses it.
- **N4b, the test copy.** The keeping sweep has its own switch (`CoachCertificates:SweepEnabled`) and runs on the Render test copy, which keeps its billing sweep off; a copy's database lives 30 days, so it never finds a line to erase. The other way: switch it off there too. Recommendation: keep.
- **N4b, a released client's read.** It answers 403, as every self-service read of a released or departed client does (the membership has ended), not the 404 the row names; nothing of the coach shows either way. No change made. Recommendation: none needed.
- **N4b, Me while the coach is read.** The waiting card keeps the plain card's height, so a card with lines grows once the read lands and moves the groups below, as N3b's badge does. The other way: a taller placeholder for everyone. Recommendation: keep; most coaches show no line yet.
- **N4b, a refusal on a line taken down meanwhile.** The page reads the lines again: the line's switch and its "Couldn’t save your choice. Try again." go, its pill says "Taken down" with the day, and focus moves to the heading, as N3b's badge page does when its badge goes. The other way: keep the refusal under the line. Recommendation: keep; there is nothing left to try again.
- **N4b, a former coach's lines on the admin's page.** A coach's clients move to the owner when the coach leaves, so no client reads a former coach's lines, and the pill takes the server's word on that: a confirmed line of a coach who left reads "Not shown: no longer coaching here", a third label the row does not name. Such a line stays confirmed and starts no keeping period until the admin takes it down. The other way: a coach's departure takes their lines down, which starts the period. Recommendation: take them down on departure, as a small row of its own, so nothing is kept without an end.
- **N4b, the lines and the badge's line.** When the coach also shows the Founding coach badge, the certificate lines come after the badge's own line, under the name and its pill. The other way: the lines first, then the badge's line. Recommendation: keep; the badge is about TB Gym, the lines about the coach.
- **N4b, the card at large text.** A card carrying lines lets the face and "Message" take rows of their own at large text, so the sentences are never squeezed to a sliver; a card without lines is exactly as before, at every size. The other way: the same wrap for every card. Recommendation: the same wrap for every card, as a small fix of its own; the plain card's name is squeezed too at 200% text.
- **N4b, a switch flipped in a loop.** Each switch gives the line a new version, and N4a's take-down carries the version the admin read, so a coach scripting the switch many times a minute could keep every take-down answering "This line changed"; the only ways out today are erasing on request (which records a request never made) or the database, since nothing in the app blocks an account. Built as N4a's rule says. The other way: a take-down that goes ahead on a line still confirmed whatever its version, since a confirmed line's words never change and only the switch can have moved. Recommendation: that change, as a small row of its own, before a coach can be told about the switch.
- **N10a, No program between programs.** On Coach Today a client whose last program is over reads "<name> has no program running" with "Joined <day>" and Assign program, and a client's page and the Messages client panel read "No program running" with "Joined <day>", as such a client read before when nothing was suggested; the item still names the latest program. The other way: "Last program ended <day>", which needs that day on the item. Recommendation: keep.
- **N10a, "On the chips too".** The statuses sheet keeps that heading over "New", now its only entry. The other way: "New" moves under the reasons and the heading goes. Recommendation: keep; N5 changes none of the sheet's words.
- **N10a, an old `/clients?show=maxes`.** A saved or shared address with the old chip opens All with nothing said, as any chip the list does not know does, and keeps `?show=maxes` until another chip is picked. The other way: a line over All saying that list is gone. Recommendation: keep; the chip showed only while some client had maxes to review or an address named it, so few saved addresses hold it.
- **N10b, the panel's suggestion lines.** The Training maxes panel no longer says that logged sets suggest a max: the sentence ("Logged sets suggest about 80 kg, up from 77.5 kg."), the set it came from and How it is worked out went with **Review the new weights** and **Keep 77.5 kg**, since together they were the review decision 19's note removed. The other way: keep the sentence as information with no button, which still tells the coach that logged sets suggest a new max. Recommendation: keep.
- **N10b, the kept line.** A keep recorded before 7 Nov 2026 is no longer shown ("You kept 77.5 kg on …, when logged sets suggested about 80 kg."); every keep stays in `strength.WorkingMaxKeeps` with its unique index, check, keys and append-only trigger, read and written by nothing. The other way: show each keep under Earlier maxes, as history. Recommendation: keep; a keep changed no max, so a lift's maxes and their history are whole without it.
- **N10b, a max applied from a suggestion.** A max a coach applied from logged sets before 7 Nov 2026 still reads "Estimated from <name>'s logged sets" on the panel, with its set's weight, reps and RPE and its day and week ("logged in the last program" when Assign program took it from the program before), and "from logged sets" in the max history: a fact about a stored max. The other way: "Estimated from a logged set", with no set. Recommendation: keep.
- **N10b, carried maxes.** Assign program still starts each lift where the client's latest program left it ("Where <program> left it, set <day>"), unless a max recorded since is newer, and the new program's max reads "Carried over from the last program" (STR-014): the coach sees a number in the box and may type another, so it is no review. The other way: start from recorded maxes alone, which drops STR-014's carry and needs the owner. Recommendation: keep.
- **N10b, How they have done.** A lift's history sheet keeps each set's "Estimated max … by the RTS chart" (or "by Epley") and the best of them (STR-016): it says what a set the client did implies and offers nothing to apply. The other way: the estimate goes too. Recommendation: keep.
- **N10b, the rule's code.** BestLoggedSet v1 (`BestLoggedSetRule.cs`, with `LoggedSetEvidence` and `LoggedSetMaxEstimate`), the reads and the keep that served it (`LoggedSetMaxSuggestions.cs`, `TrainingApplicationService.LoggedMaxes.cs`) and their reference tests were deleted, since nothing used them; git and ADR 0036 keep them, and the two domain tests that still hold (a keep's record, week 1's estimate) moved to a file of their own, `N10bStoredMaxesDomainTests.cs`. The other way: keep the code, unused, for an opt-in later. Recommendation: keep; git has the rule if suggestions ever come back as an opt-in.
- **N10b, an old `?action=maxes`.** A saved or shared address with `?action=maxes` (only Review maxes made one, and N10a removed it) opens Training as any address does and brings nothing into view. Until N10b Training took the word out of the address; now nothing reads it, so it stays there. The other way: Training takes the word out, as it does `?action=assign`. Recommendation: keep; the word does nothing and few saved addresses hold it.
- **N10b, Assign program's carried rows.** With nothing suggested, a lift carried from the last program starts folded, its line reading "Back Squat · 80 kg", as carried rows without a suggestion did and as a recorded max's row does; only a lift still to fill starts open, and no new rule opens one. The other way: open every carried row, so "Where <program> left it" shows at once. Recommendation: folded; the number is on the folded line, and opening the row shows where it came from.
- **N10c, the new second sentence.** Sunday's stage now reads "Twelve weeks of weigh-ins become one trend both of them see. When Lea changes one of Maya's training maxes, she reads every weight it moves in the coming weeks before it lands.", true of Update maxes for the coming weeks, whose sheet lists every set whose weight changes, old to new, before anything is saved; the screen-reader line names what the new picture shows (Maya's four training maxes, each typed by Lea, and the button that updates them for the coming weeks). The other way: the stage keeps its first sentence alone. Recommendation: keep; the coach's window beside it still needs a sentence that says what it is for.
- **N10c, the window.** Sunday's coach window is the same Training maxes panel, taken again from the sample demo on 7 Nov 2026. Without the suggestion the panel is shorter, so the crop keeps its width and top, shortens from 580 to 492 px to end 10 px under the panel, as it starts 10 px above it, and moves 2 px to centre it; it now shows all four of Maya's lifts (Back Squat 62.5 kg, Bench Press 35 kg, Deadlift 77.5 kg and Overhead Press 25 kg, where the suggestion box used to push the fourth out) and Update maxes for the coming weeks. The other way: another coach screen for "You both see it", the client's weight trend on the coach's side, as a new crop. Recommendation: keep; the trend is already on Maya's phone beside it.
- **N10c, the window's days.** Its lines read "Typed by you · Thu 15 Oct", where the old window read "Sat 19 Sep": a demo counts every day back from the day it is built (`DemoWorkspaceCast`), and this one was built on 7 Nov, 26 days after the one behind the week's other pictures (the hero's window reads "Monday 12 October", the phone beside this one "Last weighed 12 Oct" and "since 9 Aug"). Thu 15 Oct falls inside the week the page tells, so no two days on the page clash, though a careful reader could see the program's only maxes dated after Monday's phone shows Maya in its week 4 of 6; the stage's words name no day of the window. The other way: take the week's pictures again on one build, which this row leaves out. Recommendation: keep until the hero's morning retake (the UI plan's open follow-up), then take every picture of the week on that one build.
- **N10c, outside the row: the reduced-motion still's caption.** On a wide screen with reduced motion the stages stand still: Sunday's window is drawn 520 px wide, so it runs 31 px past its 720-px-tall figure (158 px before N10c), and the figure's "Shown with sample data" prints over the window's foot, just under Update maxes for the coming weeks and across its lower half at 200% text (before N10c just under "How it is worked out", and over it at 200%). Not changed here, since nothing else on the page changes. Recommendation: a small row of its own: in the still layout the caption sits under the pictures, as the private build's photo caption already does (`.stage__own--photo .stage__caption`), with the scene tall enough for its tallest window (Sunday's, 40 + 711 px), tested at 1440 px with reduced motion at 100% and 200% text.
- **N10c, outside the row: the Messages row's window.** The wide `messages-thread` crop (620 × 640, R6.2f's, unchanged since 13 Oct) still ends on the first words of the Messages client panel's old Needs you item, the one N10a removed: "Logged sets", "Deadlift ma" and "About 80 kg," at its right edge, the crop cutting off "suggest". At rest the phone covers that column at every wide width, phones get `thread-narrow`, which stops before it, and below about 1,330 px the window's clip hides it; with motion on, the row's entrance (both screens sliding in from transparent over 1.2 s) leaves it readable for about 0.1 to 0.25 s when a visitor pages down or flicks fast, at 1366, 1440, 1536 × 730 and 1920 px, as before N10c, and never with an unhurried wheel or reduced motion. The row's premise that the other homepage pictures show no suggestion missed it. Not changed here, since the row leaves every other picture out. The other way: clip the window nearer the phone's edge (`home-rows.scss`), a page change for a row of its own. Recommendation: take `messages-thread` and `thread-narrow` (shot `coach-messages`) again with the phone `client-messages` on one build, in the hero's morning retake: the phone needs Apple's bezel, and a window taken alone would show other days than the phone beside it.
- **N5, the Overview's links.** A client's Overview keeps its own `.panel-link` ("Open progress" and "All records" in its cards' heads, "All check-ins" beside a check-in's button), which already draws this look as a page class: the link ink underlined at 0.2em and the stronger ink on hover, but with a 2 px focus ring where the kit draws 3 px, and about 33 px tall where the kit keeps 40. The other way: move them onto `variant="link"`, one look in one place. Recommendation: move them the next time the Overview changes.
- **N5, the ⓘ's place.** Before the words, at the line's start in either direction, and left out of the button's name, which stays "How these statuses work"; only the words are underlined. The other way: after the words. Recommendation: keep; it says "help" before the words are read.
- **N5, a press.** The row names the hover alone; on a press the link look takes the stronger ink, with the kit's small dip when motion is on, and never the text look's pale fill, which would draw the box the look leaves out. The other way: the text look's fill on a press. Recommendation: keep.
- **N5, words that wrap.** At 200% text on a phone "How these statuses work" takes two lines, and the link look starts each beside the ⓘ, as a link's lines do; the kit's other buttons centre a wrapped label, which left the ⓘ 30 to 130 px from its words. The other way: centred like the kit's other buttons. Recommendation: keep.
- **N5, outside the row: Me's "Show all" of a session pack.** A client's Me ends a session pack's history with the same "Show all N" / "Show less" toggle (`features/account/client-plan.html`), still in the text look, on a phone-first screen where nothing says "tap me" either. Not changed here: the row covers Coach Today and Clients. Recommendation: the link look there too, one word and its test, as a small row of its own.
- **N5, outside the row: the phone feed's rows.** On a phone the open "What your clients did" fold draws its rows 20 px further in than its heading and the lines between its rows (the faces start 58 px from a 390 px screen's edge, Needs you's at 37), with 12 px more space above the first row: since the motion plan's W6 (a04f6f5) the rows' padding sits on `.event-inner`, so the fold's two resets in `coach-today-month.scss` reach nothing. N5's "Show all 20" sits between the two edges. Not changed here. Recommendation: point those resets at `.event-inner` and the first row's face, with a check of the rows' edge at 390 px, as a small fix of its own.
- **N6a, the words under the sixth choice.** The menu says "Last time + step"; its two boxes read "Step (kg)", with the row's "You add the step each week, after reading the logged sets." under it, and "Starting weight (kg)", with "Optional. Without one, the client picks the weight." (lb on a pound set). The other way: the row's sentence alone, under both boxes. Recommendation: keep; the second line says why the box may stay empty.
- **N6a, ✓ with the reps box empty.** Refused in the player: "Type your reps first." under the set and at the top of the workout, where focus goes as with the player's other refusals, and the box is ringed until a number is in it; nothing is sent and no rest starts. The server still records a set logged without reps (from a copy of the app opened before this change, or a request made by hand), and N6b's rule will never count one. The other way: let the set log and leave it to N6b's rule. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6a, the starting weight.** Optional. With none the session shows no weight and the player starts the weight box where TRN-029 starts any set, at last time's weight on the same set in the set's unit, else empty. The other way: a required start, or an empty weight box. Recommendation: keep; the demo's step lift has none, since one program serves clients of different strength.
- **N6a, the step's ceiling.** At most 50 in the set's unit, two decimals at most (0.01 upward). The other way: 2,000, as a typed weight. Recommendation: keep; a bigger jump is a new weight, which the coach types with Edit weeks.
- **N6a, reps on a step set.** Required (one number or a range): decision 20 adds the step only when every set reached its reps, so a step set without reps could never step. The other way: allow it, and say such a set never steps. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6a, a fixed load instead.** Refused on a step set ("A last time + step set takes no fixed load instead; type its starting weight."), and the editor never offers it there, since the override would turn the set into a typed weight and drop its step. The other way: the override wins and the step goes. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6a, choosing "Last time + step".** The cursor moves to Step, which says nothing until the coach leaves it; a typed weight the set had becomes its starting weight, and a % of training max does not (75% never becomes "start 75 kg"). Switching between the other choices keeps the box's number as before, so 75% still becomes 75 kg on "Direct load". The other way: keep the number every time, or clear it every time. Recommendation: keep, and look at % to Direct load the same way as a small fix of its own.
- **N6a, "From week 1's weight".** A step set never anchors it (TRN-026), on the server and in Assign program's sheet: its weight follows what was logged, not the week, so no max is worked out from it. The other way: count a step set with a starting weight as a typed weight. Recommendation: keep (ADR 0040, beside the maxes, not under them).
- **N6a, the lines' words.** The builder's grid, the publish preview and the week copies read "3 × 10 · last time + 2 kg · start 5 kg"; the session sheet, Edit weeks' review, Build next block's preview and a logged workout read "10 reps · 7 kg · last time + 2 kg"; the week copies and Build next block add "On a last time + step set it moves the starting weight only." when one is in the weeks. The player's Target line stays "Target: 10 reps · 7 kg": the client never sees the step (decision 21). The other way: "+2 kg a week". Recommendation: keep.
- **N6a, the demo's step lift.** The Seated Leg Curl in Lean-2's Lower A, a 2.5 kg step and no starting weight: Maya's workout today has it, as do Jad's and Nour's programs and the sample cast's (Oakline), whose next homepage pictures may show its line. The other way: a step lift for one client alone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6a, outside the row: README's "Five ways to set a load".** README.md now undercounts the weight choices; not changed, since "+ step" would promise a step the app does not apply until N6c. Recommendation: say six when N6c lands.
- **N6a, outside the row: a weight finer than a gram.** The database keeps weights to three decimals, so a typed weight, a fixed load instead or a % share sent as 0.0004 passes the server's check, is stored as 0 and refused by the database's own check, which answers 500 on Publish and a swap and a misleading 409 on Edit weeks, as before N6a (found by this row's review). N6a judges a step set's starting weight to three decimals, as the database keeps it, and its step to two; the other choices are unchanged. Recommendation: the same three-decimal judgement for every weight and share in `SetPrescriptionRules`, as a small fix of its own.
- **N6b, where the rule lives.** In Training, `LastTimeStepRule` (STR-019): a pure rule with input records of its own, filled by Infrastructure's reader from the rows, as `TrainingAttentionPolicy` is; the coach's three reads carry its answer. The other way: a rule in Strength, beside the maxes. Recommendation: keep; a step is Training's weight choice (TRN-032), not a max.
- **N6b, the step line's words.** "Last 15 kg × 10, 10, 10 on Tue · every set done · next time 17 kg", with "next time 17 kg" in ink and the rest muted, under the lift's sets in the session sheet and under the lift in a logged workout. The plan's "next time 17 kg if every set is done" was read as the reason, since the line only shows once every set was done; "every working set done" when a warm-up last time was skipped or short. Weights that differ name their sets ("next time 22.5 kg on set 2, 25 kg on sets 3–4"), and a set that keeps its weight says so after them, on a step or a hold ("set 4 is new since Tue, stays at 25 kg", "set 2 had no weight logged", "set 1 was logged without reps"). The other way: what happens next first, "Next time 17 kg · every set done, 15 kg × 10, 10, 10 on Tue". Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, the hold reasons' words.** As the plan wrote them where it did, the day named from the server's day ("today", "yesterday", "on Tue" within the week, "on Tue 3 Nov" before it): "Same weight · set 3 reached 8 of 10 on Tue", "· set 3 logged without reps on Tue", "· set 3 not logged on Tue", "· set 2 felt harder than planned on Tue (RPE 9 of 8)" (RIR when the coach wrote RIR), "· 55 kg logged on Tue looks like a typo (5.5 kg before)" ("before" rather than the plan's "the time before", since the weight it was compared with is the latest log that stood, not always the time before), "· set 2 logged in lb on Tue", "· last done Tue 13 Oct, more than 14 days ago", "Finish Monday's workout to go up" ("today's", "yesterday's", "the workout from Mon 2 Nov"), "First time · nothing logged yet to step from" and "Same weight · nothing to match on Tue". The other way: the plan's (c) and (e) words as written. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, "Keep 25 kg".** A hold of (a) or (c) keeps last time's weight (line d); when the coming set has another weight, or none (a step set with no starting weight, whose player starts at last time's), the line leads with the weight to keep instead of "Same weight", so a coach sees what N6c will offer to apply. The demo's three step lifts read this way ("Keep 25 kg · set 1 reached 11 of 12 on Tue 3 Nov"). The other way: always "Same weight". Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, the coming session's extra set.** A coming set with no match last time (a week added a set) keeps its own weight and says so, as the plan's default. The other way: it takes the last matched set's new weight. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, the 14 days.** Counted from the workout's scheduled day to today in the coming program's time zone (the same today decides which session is coming); a workout 14 days ago still counts, 15 days holds. The other way: from its finish, or the workspace's current zone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, the typo guard's comparison.** The plan's default, the latest earlier log of the set that stood (so one typo holds once and the next sensible log steps again), with one case never taken for a typo: the weight that workout asked for the set, so a step the coach applied on a light weight (5 to 7.5 kg, +50%, the judge's doubt on decision 22) never holds, and once the coach types the weight with Edit weeks the next workout logged near it ends a hold. A first log has nothing to compare and stands, even a typo, so a typo as the very first log holds the sensible logs after it until the coach types the weight. A log of 0 before is no reference. The review dropped a second case first built (a change the next log repeats is real): the player starts a step set with no starting weight at last time's weight, so a typo logged again is likely, and it now holds again until the coach types the weight, as line (e) says. The other way: also accept a change the next log repeats (a real jump the coach never typed then holds once, not until the coach types it). Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, warm-ups.** Never counted in (a), the plan's default, nor in (c), so a warm-up felt hard holds nothing; counted in the unit and typo checks, since a wrong weight there is still wrong; a warm-up step set steps by its own number only when its own last log had its reps typed (decision 22). The other way: every set in (a) and (c). Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, matching.** Writings of a lift and their sets are matched by their order (the first writing to the first, then set by set), not by their stored position, since the demo's programs count from 0 and the builder's from 1. A workout's writing is the lift's only where it was done as the coach wrote it: a writing swapped for an approved alternative keeps its place in the count (so a top set is never matched to last time's back-off) but is not read, and the lift done in another lift's place is not its history. The other way: by position, and the lift wherever it was done. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, a skipped lift.** When last time's workout was finished with the lift, or a set, not logged (or, on a step, a warm-up logged without reps), that set carries on its latest log that stood, when that was logged within the 14 days of line (b), so a skipped lift never drops a new program back to its starting weight (line d); an older log is not carried, and the set keeps its own. The other way: the set's own weight. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, a workout earlier the same day.** Counts as last time for a coming session that day. The client's player names "Last time" only from the days before. The other way: the days before, as the player. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, a forgotten Finish long ago.** A workout left running for more than 14 days reads as line (b), "last done Mon 13 Oct, more than 14 days ago", not "Finish …", since finishing it would not let the lift step. The other way: always (g). Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, which session says it.** The answer belongs to the lift's coming session only, the earliest open one across the client's programs; a later session of the same lift, or a next program while the current one still has the lift, says nothing yet. A logged workout says it only under the workout the answer read as last time, or the running one a forgotten Finish names (by its id): an older workout would describe another one's sets. The program read carries every answer, for N6c's "Apply all", and no screen shows it there yet. The other way: every session still to come, every logged workout. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, a step past 2,000.** Keeps last time's weight ("set 1 stays at 1,999 kg, as a step would pass 2,000 kg"); when no set can step the lift holds. The other way: let the answer pass the limit and N6c refuse it. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, a lift in two sessions a week.** Carry-on (line d, decision 22: "whatever its program or session name") reads the latest workout of the lift whatever its session, so a lift written with a step in two sessions of one program at different weights or reps (a heavy and a light day) is judged each week against the other day's sets: Monday's 100 kg sets read "Last 80 kg × 8, 8, 8 on Thu · every set done · next time 82.5 kg". Built as decided; found by this row's review. The other way, for a version 2: within one program, last time is the latest workout of the same weekly slot when there is one, carrying on across programs and swapped sessions only. Recommendation: version 2 as described, before coaches write a lift in two weekly sessions with a step.
- **N6b, the mocked e2e's file.** The row names `e2e/training-program.e2e.ts`, which is the client's own Training page and mocks no coach read; the coach's session sheet and logged workout are in `e2e/client-training.e2e.ts`, which gained the two N6b tests. The other way: a new coach spec under the named file. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6b, outside the row: the mesocycle read and TRN-023.** The coach's read of one program (`GET /api/training/mesocycles/{id}`) has never asked TRN-023: a lapsed, paused, blocked or released client's program reads in full there, while the program read and a logged workout close. N6b asks it only to leave its answer out (none on a closed read) and changes nothing else. Recommendation: decide whether that read should close as the others do; the session sheet, Edit weeks and Build next block read it.
- **N6b, outside the row: the program read's query pin.** The coach's program read now takes 18 queries for 24 programs, the test's ceiling (17 before N6b; its comment said 15 and now says 18); the next query on that read breaks it. Recommendation: raise it, with its reason, in the row that adds the next query.
- **N6c, "Apply all" per client.** One request applies every lift of the client whose answer moves a weight, across their programs, the row's default. The other way: per session. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6c, a hold that names last time's weight.** Offered: on a coming set at another weight (a new program's starting weight, or none yet), Apply writes last time's weight ("Keep 25 kg" offers "Apply 25 kg"), even when it is lighter than the set's own, the row's default (decision 22's carry-on). The other way: a hold applies nothing. Recommendation: keep; the button names the weight, so a lighter one is never a surprise.
- **N6c, the panel's place and words.** "Weights to apply", first on the client's Training tab while any lift's answer moves a weight, "From what Maya logged last time. Each goes to the lift's next workout." under it, and per lift its name, its workout and day ("Lower A · Tue 17 Nov") and N6b's line; nothing on a former client's record. The other way: under the timeline, or in the training maxes' column. Recommendation: keep; it is the view N6d's Coach Today line will open.
- **N6c, the buttons' words.** "Apply 17 kg" (the lightest and heaviest when the sets differ, "Apply 22.5–25 kg"), "Apply all" for two or more, "Applying…" while it goes; all outlined, since the training maxes keep the page's one filled button. The other way: "Apply" alone. Recommendation: keep; the weight on the button is what the coach approves.
- **N6c, no confirm before Apply all.** The panel lists every weight before the tap, and one tap applies them. The other way: a confirm sheet. Recommendation: keep; a weight is changed back by typing it in Edit weeks.
- **N6c, what the coach reads after an apply.** "Seated Leg Curl is now 17 kg on Tue 17 Nov. Maya sees it on that workout." on the page, or in the sheet it was applied from, which takes focus; "2 weights applied. Maya sees each on its workout." for several. The line under the lift then reads as N6b words a weight already in place (a step stays "next time 17 kg", a kept weight becomes "Same weight · …"), with no "applied" mark. The other way: "applied by Lea on Tue" on the line, one more query on the program read, whose pin is at its ceiling. Recommendation: keep; add who and when with N6d if coaches ask.
- **N6c, the client's line.** "Up 2 kg · all 3 × 10 done on Tue" (the row's words; "all 3 sets done" when the reps asked differ, "1 × 12 done" for one set, a range when the sets rose by different steps), and for a kept weight "Same weight as last time · set 1 reached 11 of 12 on Tue 3 Nov" in N6b's reason words; the lead in the accent, under the lift's Last time, outside the sets. The other way: the step up alone, nothing for a kept weight. Recommendation: keep; it tells the client what to aim for.
- **N6c, where the client reads it.** In the workout player alone, before Start and while the workout runs, the only client screen that shows a session's weights; it goes once the workout is finished, once the coach types another weight or edits that workout in Edit weeks (which rebuilds its sets), and on a swapped lift. The other way: the Training page's coming sessions too, which show counts only today. Recommendation: keep.
- **N6c, no notice to the client.** Applying sends nothing; the workout says why. The other way: the week notice a training max update sends for weeks the client can see. Recommendation: keep; coaches apply every week for every client, and a notice each time would be noise.
- **N6c, a carried-on weight of 0 kg.** A set whose answer carries on a log of 0 kg (line d) is not offered: it keeps its own weight, as no set takes 0. The other way: offer it and let the server refuse it. Recommendation: keep; found by this row's review.
