- **N9b4, a clip on a phone (the row's Open).** It plays by itself, muted and small, once near the screen, and stops off it (the default). A person who asked for reduced motion (or the app's motion switch) or for less data (Save-Data) gets the clip's first frame with a play control. The other way: tap to play everywhere. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, the pause.** Every clip that plays by itself can be stopped (WCAG 2.2.2, and the motion plan's "nothing loops without a stop"): a clip on its own (an exercise's page, the player) has its own button, "Pause the demo" or "Play the demo"; a list's thumbnails (the pool, the picker) share one "Pause demos" or "Play demos". One pause holds for every clip until the app is left; it is not kept on the device. The other way: a pause per clip, or one kept on the device. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, the player.** The shown exercise's clip sits under its name, at most 14rem wide, with its own pause; an exercise swapped for an alternative shows none, since its demos are still the prescribed exercise's (the review found the wrong movement looping under the new name); "Watch demo {n}" stays as the bigger view with the browser's controls, the way to a link and to a second demo, and its video now plays like a GIF too, muted and looping, by itself unless the clips are paused. The other way: the clip alone, without the bigger view. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, a link-only exercise in the player.** A workout names its demos without their kinds, so where an exercise's only demo is a YouTube or Vimeo link the clip's box shows for a moment, then folds away, the first time it is seen; "Watch demo" opens the link as before. In a browser with no IntersectionObserver (none in use today) such an exercise offers "Play the demo", and its press then says "This demo can’t be opened." while "Watch demo" still opens the link. The other way: the workout's reads name each demo's kind, a server change. Recommendation: keep until a row touches those reads.
- **N9b4, every coach.** The coach's demo sheet (the library's tile, the builder, Edit weeks, a client's session sheet) plays an uploaded video muted, looping and inline, by itself unless the clips are paused, its controls kept so a coach can turn the sound on; the library's tiles keep their play tile and ask no grant until pressed (R5.1b). The other way: a looping clip in every tile of the coach's library, a grant each. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, the picker.** A row whose demo is a video or a picture shows it as a square thumbnail beside the row (decorative: the row names the exercise), a link keeps the demo button that opens it on its site, and "Pause demos" sits beside the search; a video or a picture no longer opens bigger from the picker. The other way: a demo button beside each thumbnail too. Recommendation: keep; the exercise's page shows it bigger.
- **N9b4, Start a workout on an exercise's page.** Offered to a self-directed trainee alone, as Start is elsewhere: it starts a workout of one's own, adds the exercise with one empty set and opens the player; while one is unfinished it reads "Add to your workout", with "It goes into the workout you haven’t finished.", and adds it there; a start that went through with the exercise refused says so and offers "Carry on". The other way: always a new workout, refused while one is unfinished. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, the grants.** A clip asks for its grant only when near the screen; one moment's asks go in requests of six at most (each grant is a cookie of about 420 bytes in the answer, and nginx's 4 KB header buffer refuses about nine) after one antiforgery read, are kept 20 minutes, and stop at 96 live in a visit (each is a cookie for 30 minutes; the builder's library stops at the same number), past which a clip waits for a press (a list's thumbnail asks again when it next comes near, or on "Play demos"); a workspace change forgets them all; a clip whose bytes are refused (its 30 minutes ran out during a long workout) asks once more, then hides. The other way: one request per clip, which the server counts against the same allowance as set saves. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, the words the row did not name.** The pool: "TB Gym’s pool", "Exercises", "Pick an exercise to see how it’s done, then start a workout with it.", "Search by name", "Muscle", "Equipment", "No equipment", "Clear filters", "Pause demos", "Play demos", "Show more exercises", "The pool isn’t open here", "TB Gym’s exercise pool is for people training with TB Gym’s own space.", "The pool is closed for now", "No exercises yet", "No exercise matches", "Try another word, or clear the filters.", "Show all exercises"; an exercise's page: "Exercises", "See all {n} demos", "Equipment", "Movement", "Type", "Main muscle", "Also works", "How to do it", "Start a workout", "Add to your workout", "It goes into the workout you haven’t finished.", "Your workout started, but {exercise} couldn’t be added. Carry on to add it there.", "This exercise isn’t in the pool", "It may have been taken out of TB Gym’s pool, or the pool isn’t open to you.", "All exercises"; a clip: "Play the demo", "Show the demo" (for a picture), "Pause the demo", and "This demo can’t be opened." (the demo sheet's words) after a press that finds nothing; Training and Today: "Browse exercises". Recommendation: read them once; each is one line to change.
- **N9b4, the technical choices.** Web only, on N9b1's and N9b2's API. One clip component and one service serve the pool, the picker, the player and nothing else, and the demo sheet and the player's overlay keep their video with its controls; the pool's calls stay in `ExercisePoolApi` outside `ApiClient`, and the clip draws its own play and pause signs, since the kit's icons are in the first download (518.31 kB before, 518.31 kB after). The server makes no poster or rendition (PRG-008): the still is the clip's first frame. The row's e2e is a new `exercise-pool.e2e.ts`, `workout-progress.e2e.ts` for the player and the picker, and `client-training.e2e.ts` for the coach's sheet: the row's Tests line names `client-training.e2e.ts` for the player's clip, but that file is the coach's page (the mix-up N6b and N9b3 met). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b4, found outside the row: instructions in a coach's client's workout** (the row's Left out). The coach's exercise sheet says of Instructions "Clients read this in their workout, beside the demo.", and no client read carries them: only the pool's page shows an exercise's instructions now, to the house's trainees. Recommendation: a small row in which a program's exercise carries its instructions into the client's snapshot, as its demos are copied, and the player shows them under the clip; until then the sheet's line could say what is true.
- **N9b4, found outside the row: WebP in the exercise sheet.** The coach's exercise sheet offers WebP pictures (its file box takes `image/webp` and its error names WebP) while the server takes JPEG, PNG, MP4 and WebM alone, so a WebP picture is refused only once it is sent. Recommendation: a one-line fix in the sheet's types and words.
- **N9b4, "your exercises first" (N9b3's line).** The pool and the picker stay in name order: a trainee's most used first needs a server read of how often each was logged, which this web-only row does not add. Recommendation: a small server row once the pool outgrows a page.
- **N9b4, found outside the row: a batch grant behind nginx.** The batch grant answers one cookie of about 420 bytes per upload, and `src/web/nginx.conf` (the Docker stack and the Render test copy, where `/api` passes nginx; production's Caddy goes straight to the API) keeps nginx's default 4 KB response-header buffer, so an answer granting about nine uploads or more becomes a 502: the coach's exercise library asks 32 pictures at a time, older than this row. The clips ask six at a time, which fits. Recommendation: `proxy_buffer_size 16k` (and `proxy_buffers` to match) on the `/api` location, in a commit of its own, checked on the test copy.
- **N9b5, what the window cuts.** Every read of a Free trainee's own Progress: the dashboard, the weigh-ins with the chart's span, measurements and photos, each "earlier" date included, since the chart and Your entries' weigh-in list are one read. Workout history, records, a weigh-in's or a measurement's changes, a photo and a photo export stay whole, and so does every read of the house's staff. The other way: cut the charts alone and keep the lists whole, which needs a read of its own for the chart. Recommendation: keep; it is the reading of "how far back Progress reaches" the owners ticked, and nothing is locked or deleted.
- **N9b5, who is cut.** A trainee whose tier is Free and who has no paid plan of the house granting today: Pro+ or Max, and also a plan the house's staff made without a tier or a pack, once paid in full (a part grants nothing, SUB-008). A trainee who is blocked or who left reads no tier and is not cut, nor is a client the house invited on an ordinary plan. The other way: cut everyone not on a paid tier. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, the window's unit.** Whole days, 1 to 3,650, so "3 months" is entered as 90; the House page shows "90 days". The other way: months, which the dashboard's day windows would have to translate. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, the sentence (the row's Open).** "On Free, Progress shows your last {n} days. Everything you log is kept, and all of it shows here again on a paid plan." ("today alone" for 1), on Progress under the range buttons (in place of All's note, which would be untrue) and under Your entries' lede, whenever the window applies, not only once something older exists; never on a coach's read. Recommendation: the owner's words; one constant sentence per page.
- **N9b5, empty pages under the window.** Progress's weight reads "No weigh-ins in your last {n} days." and Your entries "No weights/measurements/photos in your last {n} days" with "Anything you logged before is kept, and shows again on a paid plan.", never "yet" or "your first", and "There are no earlier measurements/photos." is not said while older ones are kept. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, the range buttons.** 4 w, 12 w and All stay; a range longer than the window shows the window (on 30 days, 12 w and All both show 30 days) and the sentence says why; above 366 days All's weigh-ins reach the window while the other sections keep the dashboard's year, which a second line says. The other way: hide the ranges past the window, which puts a rule in the browser. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, a window asked for before the reach.** Answered with the reach's first day, never refused, so an old link or a page left open still loads; Your entries drops a window a refresh moved onto a newer one. The other way: refuse it with a reason. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, the house's staff read a trainee whole.** The window is what a Free trainee reads of their own Progress, not a privacy rule, so the house's Owner and Coach read every day (N9d decides later what the Owner reads). The other way: cut the staff's reads too. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, Progress's training part.** In the house, finished workouts of one's own are counted apart, by the house day each started: with nothing planned in the window a trainee reads "{n} workouts finished in the last 7 days" and "{n} workouts finished in this period" ("Workouts you finish are counted here." while none; the coach, "<name> has finished no workouts in this period."), and with sessions planned the done-of-planned bars stay and "Also {n} workouts of your own in this period" follows. A coach's client elsewhere reads the bars as before. The other way: count workouts of one's own into the bars, which would mix done-of-planned with unplanned. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, the house's storage.** Whole binary gigabytes, 1 to 10,240 (the platform's own ceiling); empty, a former house or an inactive one keeps the platform's allowance; a number below what the house holds refuses its uploads with the usual code and deletes nothing; the per-client photo allowance stays the platform's 500 MB. The House page shows neither the platform's figure nor the house's use (ADR 0014 left a usage view for later). Recommendation: a small row adding the house's use beside the setting before N9b6 loads the library.
- **N9b5, the House page's words.** "How far back Free’s Progress reaches" ("Days, from 1 to 3,650. Empty means no limit. Everything a trainee logs is kept, and a paid plan shows all of it.") and "The house’s media storage" ("Whole gigabytes, from 1 to 10,240. Empty gives the house the platform’s allowance, the same as every coaching space’s."); emptying either says what empty means for it, never that sign-up closes, and the lede now says sign-up stays closed until its two settings are filled. Recommendation: the owner's words.
- **N9b5, the technical choices.** The reach is worked out in Infrastructure from the tier read and one read of untiered paid plans, the dashboard reading the access decisions once for both; the workspace calendar says whether it is the house, so nowhere else pays a query; `reach` rides on the five own views as an optional field; the upload reads its allowance once for both checks. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b5, found outside the row: Nutrition on a trainee's Progress.** A Free trainee's Progress reads Nutrition "This is not part of the current plan." in the dashboard's coach-era words (`unavailableReason`), as N9b3 found for closed Training. Recommendation: the small words row N9b3's line asks for.
- **N9b6, the command and its words.** `house-library <the house's slug or id> <sheet.csv> [--dry-run]`: the operator names the house as `platform-admin house set` does, so a run aimed at another space, or at another deployment's database, is refused before anything is read; the clips sit beside the sheet, sub-folders allowed. The other way: no workspace word, the house found by its mark alone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, the sheet's form (the row's "TB Gym's own").** A first line naming name, type, equipment, movement, muscles, instructions and clip, in any order; each word as the API spells it or the coach's screens say it, in any case ("Horizontal push", "HorizontalPush"); the first muscle is the main one and the rest are what it also works, separated by semicolons or commas; one clip per line, required; no column for tags, swaps, Other's own type name or a second demo. The other way: those columns now. Recommendation: keep until the bought library's file list is in hand, then fit it in one small step.
- **N9b6, an exercise the sheet no longer names (the row's Open).** Left as it is: the loader only adds and updates, and archives or deletes nothing. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, who the loader acts as.** The house's Coach who joined first, else the Owner, the person a trainee's record names (ADR 0041); every clip it uploads names them as its owner. The other way: an `--as <email>` word naming a member of the house. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, a name already in the house.** The sheet's type, equipment, movement, muscles and instructions win, an empty instructions cell leaving the house's; the house's own spelling of the name, its tags, swaps and other demos stay, and a demo the library had deleted is dropped from the save. The other way: leave an exercise the house's staff made by hand alone entirely. Recommendation: keep; the sheet is the library's source.
- **N9b6, which clip is the library's.** A clip whose bytes one of the exercise's demos already has changes nothing, so a renamed file changes nothing; else the clip this loader uploaded before under the same file name (titled "<name> (library clip)") is replaced in its place and deleted as the library deletes one (kept 30 days, for ever while a program holds it); else the clip goes first, the demo the pool plays, refused when the exercise holds ten demos. A demo the house's staff uploaded is never replaced or deleted, even under the same file name; a library clip renamed and re-cut becomes a second demo. The other way: replace the first video whoever uploaded it. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, archived exercises and swaps.** A line naming an archived exercise is refused, never restored; a line that would change an exercise whose swap is archived is refused before anything is uploaded, since the library will not save it, and the line names the swap. The other way: restore the exercise, or drop the swap. Recommendation: keep; both are the staff's to undo.
- **N9b6, when a run stops.** A full storage allowance, uploads off (storage or scanner), nobody to load as or another upload under way stops the run at that line, counted as refused, with "Not reached: n" and what to wait for ("once the house's media storage has room", "once uploads work again"); exit 0 when every line loaded or was unchanged, 1 when a line was refused or the run stopped, 2 before line one; a dry run still checks the lines where uploads are off and says so. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, the house's use (N9b5's line).** The run and the dry run open with "The house stores X of its Y allowance.", and the dry run ends with what it would upload and, when that is more than the room left, says a run would stop at the first clip that does not fit. The House page still shows neither. Recommendation: enough to load the library; the House page row stays optional.
- **N9b6, the technical choices.** The sheet is read by a small CSV reader of the ExerciseLibrary module's own, so no package is added; each call runs in a fresh scope as one person, the way the demo generator's requests run; the library's own rules run in memory before an upload so a refused line uploads nothing, and a clip uploaded for a save the library then refuses is deleted again; the allowance's measure gained a static read beside the upload's. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9b6, found outside the row: a server without photos has no demos either.** GO-LIVE's 4 GB choice "without progress photos" sets both media adapters to None, which turns off every upload, a coach's uploaded exercise demos and the house's library included (a YouTube or Vimeo link still works), not only photos; the loader then stops before line one. Recommendation: say so beside that choice in GO-LIVE when the server is picked; the library needs the photos setup (R2 and ClamAV).
- **N9e, who may close (the row's Open).** Every client, on their Me, whether a house trainee or a coach's client, since the operation is the same and the checklist's privacy item is for everyone (the row's default); someone with no coaching space left, who cannot open Me, finds the same on Password and security. An account that owns or coaches a space, or ever did, and a platform admin's are refused by the server ("This account owns or coaches a coaching space on TB Gym, or once did, so it can't be closed here."). Me hides the button from someone who is staff in a space they belong to now, but the web knows only current memberships, so a former coach still sees it and is refused after typing the password. The other way: house trainees only. Recommendation: keep; tell a former coach before the dialog with a flag on the account read in a later row.
- **N9e, what the client's coach is told (the row's Open).** The notice a leaving client raises today, to the owner and the coach, and nothing more; a self-directed trainee's leaving still tells nobody (CLI-026). The name is erased in the same transaction, so the notice reads "Closed account left your coaching space", and Former clients lists "Closed account · Left by client" with the reason "The client closed their TB Gym account.". The other way: a notice of its own, or naming the person once before the erasure (which keeps their name in the coach's inbox for good, since a notice is never rewritten). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9e, the password asked again.** Closing asks for the account's password in its dialog and the server checks it, so a session left open on a shared phone cannot erase the person; a wrong one is said under the field, which takes focus, and counts towards no lock, as Change your password does. The other way: a typed word such as CLOSE, or wrong passwords counting towards the sign-in lock. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9e, where the button sits.** On Me, the last thing on the page, under Sign out, under its own small heading "Close your account" with one quiet line, as a text button "Close your TB Gym account". On Password and security only for someone with no coaching space left, as a panel like the page's others ("Erase your details from TB Gym for good. You can't undo this."). The other way: Password and security for everyone, beside Leave. Recommendation: keep; the row says Me.
- **N9e, the dialog's words.** "Close your TB Gym account?", "You leave every coaching space you're part of, and any coach who coaches you is told you left.", what goes ("Erased: your name, email, phone and date of birth, everything you answered when you joined, health details included, and your progress photos."), what stays ("Kept, without your name: your plans and payments, your workouts and records, the figures your meal plans were worked out from, and what you logged or wrote: weigh-ins, check-ins, notes and messages."), "You can't undo this. You can sign up again with the same email later, as a new person.", the password, "Keep my account" and "Close my account"; then sign-in says "Your TB Gym account is closed and your details are erased." Recommendation: the owner's and, for the promise, the lawyer's words.
- **N9e, the anonymous record.** Every client record of the person, current or ended, in every space, keeps its ids, coach, dates and every fact hung on it, and reads "Closed account" with an address of its own under the reserved `closed.tbgym.invalid` (no mail can reach it); its phone, date of birth, height, every intake answer, the health fields and the coach's notes are emptied, the client's declared allergens deleted, and the invitations they accepted lose what the coach typed. The former client's page still shows that address and still says "You can invite them back as a new client". The other way: an empty address, which every read of a client assumes is there. Recommendation: keep the data; a line on the former client's page saying the account was closed, in a small words row.
- **N9e, what is kept under the anonymous record.** Beyond the row's list (payments, enrollments, finished workouts, records): maxes, messages, check-in answers, workout notes, weigh-ins and measurements (their append-only corrections too), meal plans, food logs and the calculation snapshots that hold age, sex, weight and height, the relationship events' reasons, staff notes on payments, and an unfinished workout, which stays unfinished. Free text among them may hold a name or a health detail. The other way: blank that free text too, which needs a defined change to several append-only tables. Recommendation: the lawyer's list decides, with the retention periods.
- **N9e, notices that named the client before.** A staff notice raised before the closing ("Mira Haddad asked to renew", "… left a note on …") keeps the name: a delivered notice is a snapshot the database refuses to change. The other way: a defined rewrite of those titles at closing. Recommendation: ask the lawyer; the rewrite is a small row if needed.
- **N9e, invitations not accepted.** An invitation a coach sent to the person's email and never accepted by them keeps its address and names (it is the coach's, and the address may belong to a new account later). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9e, the platform admin's way (the row's "on a request by hand").** `POST /api/platform-admin/accounts/close` with the email and the day the person asked, recorded as the admin's closing on request; no screen and no `platform-admin` command (the row says Server and Me). Until a screen exists the admin calls the route by hand. Recommendation: a small "Close an account on request" sheet on the admin's pages in a later row.
- **N9e, coming back after closing.** The email signs up or is invited again as a new person with a new account: nothing of the old one follows, so a client the house ended or blocked can join the house again after closing (CLI-024 refuses by account). The other way: keep a one-way fingerprint of closed addresses to refuse them, which is personal data kept after an erasure. Recommendation: keep, the lawyer to confirm.
- **N9e, photos.** Closing skips the 30-day recoverable window: every photo of the person, one removed earlier included, falls due for the next purge sweep, and the file name their phone gave it is forgotten. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9e, how long each kind is kept (the row's Left out).** Not built: the retention periods are the lawyer's list. Recommendation: build them as settings once the list exists.
- **N9e, the technical choices.** One database transaction across every space through a scope a space whose context joins the account's connection, a waiting sign-up's lock and then the account's taken first; the account row and every client record stay (append-only records name them) and are emptied, never deleted; the freeze and invitation triggers let the anonymising through once and checks keep an anonymised row anonymous; deferred triggers refuse a new membership, client record or upload for a closed account at commit, so a join or upload under way cannot put the person back; one append-only `identity."AccountClosures"` row a closing. The migration's revert puts back the old intake check, which an anonymised record fails, so a revert after a real closing needs that record dealt with first. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, the house's Whish number (the row's Open).** A house setting the platform admin enters in the app (`WhishNumber`, the row's default), kept as typed with single spaces and an optional + (6 to 20 digits, 40 characters at most); empty keeps asks closed. The other way: a configuration key beside `PlatformBilling:WhishNumber`, which needs a deployment to change. Recommendation: keep. Its line on the House page is N9c2's; until then the page leaves it off rather than fail on it.
- **N9c1, whether an ask lapses (the row's Open).** Never: an ask stays open until the house's staff book it or close it with a reason, which is kept and which the trainee reads on their page. The other way: an ask that lapses after some days. Recommendation: keep while the staff are two partners.
- **N9c1, who may ask.** A self-directed trainee of the house alone; a client the house invited, and a coach's client in their coach's space, read no plans and are answered as if the page did not exist. The other way: let an invited client of the house ask too. Recommendation: keep; an invited client deals with the staff who invited them.
- **N9c1, Max.** Not offered and refused until Max's places exist (N9c5), since an empty number of places keeps Max closed and every part stays switched off until the owner turns it on. The other way: let Max be asked for as soon as the Whish number is filled. Recommendation: keep; N9c5 opens it.
- **N9c1, one ask at a time.** A trainee has one open ask: asking for the same option again answers the open ask, and any other option, of that plan or another, is refused ("You already asked for Pro+ Programs, 4 weeks. …") until the staff book or close it. The trainee cannot withdraw an ask, and on Free has no Messages to ask the staff to close one. The other way: a second ask replacing the first, or a Withdraw button. Recommendation: a Withdraw on N9c2's page while nothing was said sent; the staff close the rest.
- **N9c1, "I've paid" once.** The money of an ask is said sent once, with the Whish name and number; the same details again answer the first, other details are refused ("TB Gym looks for it with the details you gave first"), and nothing more is said on an ask already booked or closed. A trainee on Free has no way in the app to tell the staff the details were wrong. The other way: a correction that replaces the details. Recommendation: decide on N9c2 whether a trainee may correct the details before the staff book; a wrong number is rare.
- **N9c1, the day a plan is booked from.** The house's day the staff record the money, or the day the last plan it cannot run beside ends on, when later: the trainee's other Pro+ or Max, and any plan holding one of its features alone (an untiered Training plan the staff sold, even one still waiting for its money, but not an add-on covering other features, nor Free or a cancelled plan); the staff do not pick the day, and the receipt is dated that day too. The other way: a received-on day the staff type, as the coaches' own receipt sheet has. Recommendation: keep, so no paid day passes before the money is recorded.
- **N9c1, an ask whose plan went off sale meanwhile.** It can still be booked, at the price asked for, since the trainee sent that money. The other way: refuse it and ask again. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, the receipt.** A mobile-wallet receipt of the exact price, with the staff's own optional reference; the trainee's Whish name and number are not copied onto it, since a receipt can never be changed and closing the account must be able to erase them. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, no plan notice to the trainee.** Booking a plan raises none of the four plan notices (waiting for payment, started, ending soon, ended), whose words speak of "your coach". The other way: house-worded notices (the plan has started; it ends soon, ask for the next one). Recommendation: write the house's words, then add them in a small row; the ending-soon one helps the next pass.
- **N9c1, who is told.** The house's Owner and the trainee's coach, once each, the same person once; a second Coach of the house is not told and cannot open the trainee (ADR 0026). The other way: every member of the house's staff. Recommendation: keep until N9d's privacy split.
- **N9c1, a blocked trainee.** Cannot ask (the page offers nothing) and the staff cannot book for them; they may still say an earlier ask's money was sent. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, a plan priced at nothing.** A Pro+ option priced at 0 is not offered and cannot be asked for, since nothing would be recorded; the staff give it directly. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, Ask to renew.** A self-directed trainee never asks to renew the coaches' way, whatever their paid plan is doing: their next pass is asked for the house's way, so one pass is never asked for twice. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, prices on a client's read.** SUB-017 keeps prices off a client's plan read; the house's plans on sale carry theirs on a read of their own, which the trainee needs to pay. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, the words the row did not name.** The notices "<name> asked for a plan" ("Open the client's page to see the ask. Book the plan once the money is there.") and "<name> says the money is sent" ("Check the Whish account, then book the plan from the client's page."), and each refusal's sentence (asks closed: "TB Gym isn't taking asks for a paid plan right now. Nothing you have changes."; not on sale; another ask open, naming its plan and option; money said with other details; ask already booked or closed; the exact price with its amount; the plan's currency). The read says asks are closed for a blocked trainee as for an empty Whish number, with no field telling the two apart. Recommendation: read them once on N9c2's screens, and add the reason to the read there if the page needs it.
- **N9c1, the technical choices.** Three append-only tables in the Subscriptions module (the ask, the word on the money, the outcome), each write under the trainee's profile row lock; a booking writes the plan active, its receipt and the outcome in one transaction under one key, never through the two staff routes; the database checks that a booking names the trainee's own plan of the option asked for and a receipt of its whole price, keeps asks in the house alone and for self-directed trainees, and keeps a trainee's Pro+ and Max plans apart (an exclusion); the word on the money takes one change ever, its erasure when the account closes. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c1, outside the row: the House page and a new setting.** The House page failed on a setting it had no words for, so a server row adding one broke it until the web caught up; it now leaves such a setting off. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c2, Free on My plan (N9b1's line).** A trainee's My plan shows Free as a card of its own: "No end date", "Since <day>" and "It never ends, and it keeps running beside any plan you add.", never a last day or days left; the coach's empty line "Your coach will put your plan here." no longer reaches a trainee, and a paid plan's notes say TB Gym where they said your coach ("You’re back on Free. Everything you logged stays yours." once one ends). The other way: Free listed as a plan with its dates. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c2, the page's order.** Top to bottom: the ask that waits on the trainee, their paid plans, Free, Plans you can add, Earlier asks. The other way: Free first, as the plan they always have. Recommendation: keep; what waits on them comes first.
- **N9c2, a confirm before asking.** "Ask for 4 weeks" opens a sheet with the option, its length and price and what happens next, and "Ask for it" sends the ask, since an ask cannot be withdrawn. The other way: one tap asks. Recommendation: keep while there is no Withdraw.
- **N9c2, Withdraw and correcting the Whish details (N9c1's lines).** Both need a server route, which a web-only row does not build, so neither exists: the sheet says "You can ask for something else once TB Gym books or closes this ask.", the I've-paid sheet "You can say this once for this ask.", and the staff close an ask with a reason. Recommendation: a small row for a Withdraw while nothing was said sent; leave corrections out, a wrong number being rare.
- **N9c2, the words of each state (the row's Open).** The trainee reads "Waiting for your payment" (with "Send USD 47.50 by Whish to <number>, then tell TB Gym you sent it." and Copy), "Money sent" ("You said you sent it on <day> from <name>, <number>."), then under Earlier asks "Booked" ("Booked <day>: runs <dates>.") or "Closed" ("TB Gym closed this ask on <day>." and the reason); the staff read "Waiting for the money", "Says it’s sent", "Booked" and "Closed", with who decided. The other way: one set of words for both. Recommendation: read them once on the pictures.
- **N9c2, when a plan starts, in the trainee's words.** "A plan starts once TB Gym has your money, or when your current plan ends if that is later. Free keeps running underneath.", the server's booking day (N9c1) in the trainee's words, since a trainee whose Pro+ runs may ask for the next pass. The other way: two sentences chosen by whether a paid plan runs. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c2, plans while an ask is open or asks are off.** Plans you can add is left off while an ask is open (the server refuses another) and while asks are switched off, with nothing said about why (the row's "Free alone"); a blocked trainee reads the same, since the read does not tell the two apart. The other way: the plans shown without their buttons and a sentence. Recommendation: keep; add the reason to the read only if testers ask.
- **N9c2, the Whish number on the trainee's page.** Shown on the ask that waits for the money, with Copy, and not beside the plans before anyone asks. The other way: in Plans you can add's lines. Recommendation: keep; the number matters once there is something to pay.
- **N9c2, the booking sheet.** "Book the plan" shows the plan, what the trainee said they sent, the amount prefilled with the exact price (editable, any other figure refused under the field in the server's words) and an optional reference, and no day: the server books on the house's day. The sheet and the closing dialog cannot be shut while their answer is on its way. The other way: an amount the staff cannot change. Recommendation: keep, so the staff confirm what arrived.
- **N9c2, the staff's panel.** "Asks for a plan" sits under the current plan on a house trainee's Service page, only once they have asked; Book plan is outlined and Close ask a text button, so the page keeps its one main action; a former trainee's asks are listed with no buttons. The other way: always shown, with a line when nobody asked. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c2, the notices and the House page.** Both notices open the trainee's Service page, as Ask to renew's does; undelivered, they read "An ask for a plan" and "A trainee saying they paid". The House page's line reads "Whish number for plans", shown as stored, left to right; emptied, "… is now empty, so asks for a paid plan are closed." Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c2, the technical choices.** The web API client regenerated from N9c1's server; the trainee's and the staff's reads in two small services outside the app's eagerly loaded client (the first download); the house's plans read only when My plan's read carries Free (a coach's client never calls it) and the staff's asks only for a client with a Free plan; the booking's key bound to its payload, so a retry sends the same key; My plan's styles now compile to 7.49 kB, past a screen's 5 kB warning (as eleven other screens are) and under its 8 kB error. Recommendation: keep, and move the house's part of My plan into a component of its own in the next row that adds to the page (N9c4).
- **N9c2, outside the row: Free's buttons on the client's page.** N9b1's line asks this row to mark Free as TB Gym's own and hide its Manage plan menu; the row's third column does not name it, so a trainee's Service page still offers Renew, Pause and Cancel on Free, which the server refuses (409 `house_free_plan`). Recommendation: a small web row before the house's staff work there daily.
- **N9c3, how blocks are ordered (the row's Open).** The house's staff give a program a run's name and its place in it (1 to 52) when they offer it; runs are matched by name, whatever the spaces and capitals; one offered program a place; a program with no run starts whenever the rest allows. A run renamed later keeps its trainees going: the block before is the program offered at the place before, whatever the run is called now, or one started under the run's name. The other way: an ordered list of programs a run, kept apart from each program. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, a later block first (the row's Open).** Never: block 2 opens once the trainee started block 1 and that program's dates have ended, finished or not, or its pass was cancelled; a block the staff cancelled while its pass ran does not count; starting block 1 again is allowed; with block 1 withdrawn and never started, block 2 waits ("block 1 isn't offered now"). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, a block finished early.** The next block still waits for the day after the finished block's last day, because a finished program keeps its dates (TRN-013: only a cancelled one frees them, in every space). With sessions startable any day of their week, a trainee loses at most the rest of the block's last week. The other way: a finished block frees its dates, a change to that rule for every coaching space. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, the day a block starts.** The house's day Start is pressed; the trainee does not choose a later day. The other way: any day up to the last day it can start. Recommendation: keep; the current week's sessions start any day anyway.
- **N9c3, which plan Start uses.** Pro+ alone, the one that grants that day; a pass booked to begin later is used from its first day; Max's program is the house's Coach's pick (N9c5); Free and an untiered plan of the house never. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, "the current week" (TRN-020).** The program's own week: the seven days from the week's first day, counted from the day the block started (TRN-007), not the house's calendar week, which would cut a block started mid-week in two. A session started early is on Today while its workout is open and on the day it was done, and "last time" counts it from that day. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, every week shown.** Start publishes every week of the trainee's copy, even a week the program keeps unpublished by default, since nobody opens weeks for a trainee. The other way: those weeks stay hidden for good. Recommendation: keep, and say so in N9c4's marking form.
- **N9c3, the rounding.** Each program on the list carries the rounding a trainee's weights take, 2.5 to the nearest by default, as Assign program offers a coach; the unit is the program's own, and a program mixing kg and lb cannot be offered. Recommendation: keep; N9c4's form can leave the defaults.
- **N9c3, what a trainee types.** Their training max, the number a program's percentages read: more than 0, at most 500 kg or 1,100 lb, two decimals, dated that day, for any lift of the house (an archived one too, which a program may still use), under Pro+ only, named "typed by the trainee" in the coach's max history; no read of their own max history (each program's maxes are in the programs read). The other way: they type a tested max and the program takes 90% of it (TrainingMaxShare, as a max from a set does). Recommendation: keep for Pro+; revisit with Pro (Step 5).
- **N9c3, the max a lift starts from.** The newer of the maxes recorded for them in the program's unit (a training max first) and their last program's max in use, as Assign program offers a coach (STR-014): a later day wins, and on the same day the one recorded later; an exact tie goes to the last program's. A typed max never changes a block already running (STR-002); it reaches the next one. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, a pass that stops granting mid-block.** Cancelled, paused or blocked: the block's sessions shut (start, log, swap and notes answer `program_plan_closed`), Today, Upcoming and the week leave them out, Training's program read still shows the block, and a workout already started may still be finished with what was logged; a workout of one's own still opens. The other way: shut the finish too. Recommendation: keep; N9c4 says why on the page.
- **N9c3, a cancelled pass's block.** It holds no days for the next program: the trainee's next Start closes it (kept as cancelled with the reason "Its Pro+ pass was cancelled"), unless a workout of it is still open, which they are asked to finish first; for the run it counts as ended on the day the pass was cancelled, so the next block opens under the next pass. The other way: close it when the staff cancel the pass. Recommendation: keep; nothing to do by hand.
- **N9c3, the gate is the house's alone.** In a coaching space a program's sessions still open whenever Training is open, as before. The other way: every space follows the plan a program sits under. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, withdrawing a program.** It leaves the trainees' list and Start refuses it; what trainees started stays theirs; its place in the run is free for another. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c3, the words the row did not name.** The refusals' sentences: "Starting a program needs Pro+. Free keeps running, with everything you logged.", "Block 2 of <run> opens on <day>, once block 1 has ended.", "This is block 2 of <run>. Start block 1 first.", "This is block 2 of <run>, and block 1 isn't offered now, so it can't start yet.", ""<program>" runs until <day>. One program at a time: this one can start from <day>.", "Finish the workout you started in "<program>" first: its plan has ended, so it closes once that workout is done.", "This block runs 4 weeks and your pass ends on <day>, so the last day to start it was <day>. Start it under your next pass.", "This block runs 6 weeks, longer than your pass, so it can't start under it.", "Type your max for <lifts> first, in kg: this program works its weights out from it.", "TB Gym changed this program since you read it. Read it again, then start it.", "TB Gym no longer offers this program. Pick another from the list.", "This program came with a plan that isn't running now, so its sessions are closed. What you logged stays yours."; the staff's ""<program>" is already block 2 of <run>. Give this one another place, or withdraw that one first."; a max's "Typing your max is for a program, which needs Pro+." and "Enter a max above 0 and at most 500 kg (1,100 lb), with two decimals at most."; the program's history lines "Started by the trainee from the house's programs." and "Its Pro+ pass was cancelled; closed when the trainee started another program.". Recommendation: read them on N9c4's pictures.
- **N9c3, the technical choices.** Two tables of the Training module (the staff's list entries, mutable under a row version; the trainee's starts, append-only, checked by a trigger to name their own program of that version under their own Pro+ plan), both kept in the house by triggers; Start under the trainee's profile row lock, through the same snapshot step as Assign program, its key bound to the trainee, the entry and the version; the plan checks in one helper reading the commercial records, so Training references no other module; the migration's revert stops once a trainee started a program. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, where the page is reached.** From Training alone: "TB Gym's programs" beside Start a workout and Browse exercises when no program runs, and at the foot of a running program after "See what's next"; Today has no link. The other way: Today's start card too. Recommendation: keep; add it to Today if the test's trainees miss it.
- **N9c4, Ask for Pro+.** On Free a link to My plan, where N9c2's ask, the Whish number and I've paid already are; with an ask open the card says "You asked for <plan>. My plan shows where it stands." and links there; with asks switched off, "Pro+ can't be asked for yet." The other way: the ask sheet on the programs page, with each Pro+ option. Recommendation: keep: one place to ask and pay.
- **N9c4, the page's words.** "Programs", "Programs written by TB Gym's coach. You follow one at a time."; the pass card "Pro+ until <day>" and "<program> runs until <day>." with Open Training; a card's "Block 1 of <run>", "For: <whom it suits>", "<n> weeks · kg", the description, a "Week 1" fold ("Day 1 · Lower", "Back squat · 3 × 5 · main lift", "<n> sets" when no reps are set), "Works its weights out from your max for:" with each lift's "140 kg, typed <day>", "from your last program" or "not typed yet"; "No programs yet" when none is offered; a trainee never reads how many started a program. Recommendation: read them on the pictures.
- **N9c4, Start.** A question first: "Start <program>?", "It runs <n> weeks, from today until <day>. One program at a time: TB Gym's other programs wait until it ends.", with "Start program" and "Not now"; then "<program> started. Its sessions are on Today and Training." and Training opens. Every card that can start has a filled Start. The other way: no question. Recommendation: keep the question: a Start holds the trainee's days.
- **N9c4, typing one's maxes.** A sheet, "Your maxes", one box a lift in the program's unit with its words ("Your training max: the weight the program's percentages read."), filled with the max it would start from; only a changed lift is sent, one request each, never retried, checked in the browser as the server checks it; under Pro+ alone; an empty box sends nothing, since a max is never removed. Once a block runs, its lifts read "from your last program" (N9c3's pick names the program in use so). The other way: boxes on the card itself. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, the refusals.** The read carries a refusal's code and days but no sentence, so the page writes N9c3's sentences from them; one sentence changed: an open workout under an ended plan reads "A workout you started in "<program>" is still open, and its plan has ended, so another program can't start yet." (see the line on that workout below). A Start refused for a cause the page did not know reads the list again under "Your programs changed since you read them. Here they are again." ("Your Pro+ pass isn't running now, so this program couldn't start." when the pass stopped). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, own workouts beside a program.** Offered on Training once no program runs (none, one finished, or one whose plan closed), with "Start from this workout" under the same rule; hidden while a program runs, as N9b3 built. The other way: offered beside a running program too. Recommendation: keep; look again with Pro (Step 5).
- **N9c4, Start now.** A session of the program's current week still to come has "Start now" on Training, without Do it now's question, and the player opens on it; the hero's "Started <day>" is the day it was started. Do it now's question for a trainee says "It was planned for <day>. Done now, it counts as done late." without "your coach". The other way: ask before Start now too. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, a closed plan on Training.** The hero says N9c3's sentence instead of its action and every session row is plain, read from Upcoming having no program (the server's own gate), since the program read carries no flag. The player, refused a set, swap or note with `program_plan_closed`, says the same sentence without reading the workout again, and puts sets typed but not logged back to what the server holds so Finish is not held up. The other way: a flag on the program read (a server change). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, the coach's section.** "Programs trainees can start" under the Programs page's list, shown only in the house: each row's Offered or Withdrawn pill, its block, "For: …", weeks and unit, "Loads rounded to 2.5 kg, nearest", "Started once" or "Not started yet", "Archived: trainees don't see it.", a warning when it mixes kg and lb; Edit; Withdraw at once with a toast, "<program> withdrawn. Trainees who started it keep their copy.", no Undo (the row then offers Offer again); "Offer a program" outlined, disabled with its reason when nothing is left to offer. The sheet, "Offer to trainees" or "Edit for trainees": Program, Whom it suits, Run (optional), Block number, Rounding folded at 2.5 to the nearest, and "A trainee's copy shows every week, even weeks not shared with clients. Trainees also read the program's description." The other way: the marking in the builder. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, how the web knows the house.** It does not: the coach's Programs page reads the house's list once a workspace and session, and its 404 hides the section, so a coach elsewhere has one 404 line in the browser's console. The other way: a house mark on `/api/tenants` (a server change). Recommendation: add the mark in the next server row that touches workspaces.
- **N9c4, the technical choices.** The web API client regenerated from N9c3's server; the trainee's and the staff's reads in two small services outside the eagerly loaded client; Training reads the client's profile whether or not a program runs (one more read for every client opening Training) and Upcoming for a trainee's running program; `tier` on My plan's offers; first download 518.35 kB before and after. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c4, outside the row: an open workout under an ended plan.** No read lists a workout started in a program whose plan then stopped (Today and Upcoming leave that program out), so the trainee cannot open it to finish it, though the server would let them, and while it stays open every Start is refused until the block's dates end. The web promises nothing it cannot do (the row is plain and the sentence says what stands). Recommendation: a small server row before Pro+ is switched on: Today or Upcoming keeps that workout, finish-only.
- **N9c4, outside the row: "your coach" in the player.** A trainee on one of TB Gym's programs still reads the player's "Note for your coach", "Send note" and "Your coach set these loads", though their notes reach no inbox. Recommendation: a small web row: the player reads the profile as Training now does.
- **N9c4, outside the row: publishing a program the house offers.** The builder does not say that publishing a new version changes what the next trainee to start it copies, and calls the description "for your team" though trainees read it. Recommendation: a line for the house in the builder's publish preview and the description's help.
- **N9d1a, which side a read falls on (the row's Open).** Business, the Owner's for every client: a client's name, email, phone, coach, onboarding status, block, release and version; the plan, its enrollments, payments, sessions and packs, renewals and plan asks; the coach history; block and unblock; release; on Clients, Today and a client's page the plan, the status, the counts of sessions scheduled and done, of check-ins waiting for review and overdue (with the overdue one's due day), of sessions missed, and when a check-in was last sent. Private by default, only the client's own coach: everything under Training, Nutrition, Strength, Progress and Check-ins (the check-in list itself included, though it holds no answers), the intake answers and the health fields, the birth date, height, work and steps, the coach's notes, the last activity (its kind, time and session name), the last workout and its day, the feed's workouts and check-ins, Week not shared, No program and Weights to apply. The other way: a read moved to business where the partners want the owner to see it (No program is the likeliest). Recommendation: keep, and read the list on N9d2's pictures.
- **N9d1a, Today for another coach's client.** The owner's Needs you keeps another coach's client's plan, payment and renewal items and the counts of check-ins waiting and of missed sessions, so the owner can oversee the coaches, but a check-in to review opens nothing (its check-in is withheld), missed sessions do not say when the client last trained, and the feed leaves the client out. The other way: the owner's Needs you lists only what the owner can act on (the plan, payment and renewal kinds). Recommendation: keep until N9d2 shows it; if it crowds the owner's own work, N9d2 can group another coach's items under that coach.
- **N9d1a, check-ins to review and client targets.** Both lists now hold the caller's own clients for the Owner as for a Coach (each item opens the answers or the targets); until today the Owner's held every coach's (the owner's choice of 28 Oct 2026, CHK-014). The other way: keep listing every coach's for the Owner with nothing to open. Recommendation: keep: the counts stay on Today and the client's page.
- **N9d1a, a former client.** The private side of a released or departed client opens only to an owner who was its coach when it ended; the coach the client left loses the whole record (ADR 0027), so another coach's former client's private side is read by nobody, and the owner, who keeps the record, reads its business side alone (a private change answers 404, not 409). Releasing a coach's client therefore opens nothing. The other way: the record's keeper reads it whole, as before. Recommendation: keep: otherwise a release would be a way around the split.
- **N9d1a, an owner taking a client.** An owner who reassigns a coach's client to themselves becomes its coach and opens its private side, and handing it back closes it again; until N9d1b the client is told of no reassignment (as today), so the coach history, which only the owner reads, is the one trace. C8 says every coach change is told to the client: N9d1b builds that notice for every reassignment, not only for a waiting client. The other way: build the notice in this row (a new notice kind with a migration of the notices' vocabulary). Recommendation: keep it in N9d1b, the next row.
- **N9d1a, a coach leaving, until N9d1b.** Removing a coach or a coach resigning still moves their clients to the owner, who then coaches them and reads their private side; N9d1b makes them wait for a coach instead. Recommendation: build N9d1b next, as the plan's order says.
- **N9d1a, another coach's client's name and phone.** The owner cannot correct them: the only route that changes them, the intake's, carries the health fields and is private. The other way: a business route for a client's name, email and phone. Recommendation: a small server and web row after N9d2 if the partners ask for it.
- **N9d1a, the house.** The house's Owner (the tech partner) now reads a trainee's private side only for trainees assigned to them; trainees confirmed while the house has a Coach are the Coach's (CLI-024), so the Coach (the trainer partner) reads their logs and the Owner their plans and payments (decision 33 with C8). The other way: none; C8 applies to every space. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d1a, until N9d2.** The owner's screens still offer what the server now refuses on another coach's client (its Training, Nutrition, Progress and Check-ins tabs answer as missing, and the client's details come without the intake), so the owner reads refusals there until N9d2; the server is right meanwhile. Recommendation: build N9d2 after N9d1b, as the plan's order says.
- **N9d1a, the technical choices.** One SharedKernel marker (`ClientSide.Business` and `ClientSide.Private`) on each route group or route, read by the tenant handler only when the route names a client, a route without one counting as private, the business list pinned by a test; `CoachClientScope` keeps its one membership read a request and adds `ExcludesFromManagingAsync`, `ExcludesFromCoachingAsync`, `MayCoachAsync` and `StaffUserIdAsync`; a former client's coach at the end is read from the history (a `Released` or `ClientLeft` entry means someone else coached them), so nothing new is stored; the mixed reads withhold their private fields as null with `MayCoach` false (`CoachClientDetails`, `CoachClientRef`); the overview reads a program, its weeks and the lifts' standings only for the clients the caller coaches; Coach attention stays v9, since only the clients its private signals are read for changed; no migration; the web API client is not regenerated (N9d2 does). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d1b, a house trainee with no house Coach (the row's Open).** Built as the row's default: they wait for a coach (no coach on the record, a `SignUp` entry naming nobody), so nobody on the house's team opens their training until the Owner gives them one, and they train, log and ask for plans as before; a house Coach leaving leaves their trainees waiting the same way. The other way: the Owner keeps them, as before. Recommendation: keep; decision 33 has the trainer partner join the house's team before sign-up opens, so a trainee waits only if that Coach leaves.
- **N9d1b, who an Ask to renew tells while the client waits (the row's Open).** Built as the row's default: the owner, stored as the one told (no schema change), and the owner's notice is suppressed if the client is given a coach before it is delivered, as a coach's is today. The other way: tell nobody until a coach is given. Recommendation: keep; renewals are the business side (C8).
- **N9d1b, clients the owner holds from an earlier departure (the row's Open).** Built as the row's default: they stay the owner's (nothing is rewritten; the history's old departure entries still name the owner). The other way: a one-off step that leaves them waiting. Recommendation: keep; before launch there are none outside the demos.
- **N9d1b, the words of the notices.** The client whose coach left reads "Your coach has left. Your coaching space will give you a new coach and tell you when it does. Your plan and history stay as they are." (version 3; its email is unchanged); the owner, when a coach resigns, "A coach left your team. Their clients now wait for a coach, as will anyone who accepts one of their invitations. Give each one a coach from Clients, yourself included." (version 3); a client given a coach "You have a new coach. Open Today to see who coaches you now. Your plan and history stay as they are." (new). None names anyone (NOT-006). The other way: name the new coach, which NOT-006 would need an exception for. Recommendation: keep naming nobody; the client's Today says who.
- **N9d1b, a self-directed trainee whose record changes hands.** C8 says every coach change is told, but a trainee trains as their own coach (CLI-026), so "You have a new coach" would not be true: they are told in words of their own, "Who can see your training changed. Another member of your coaching space's team can now see your training and progress. Your plan and history stay as they are." (a second new notice). A coach leaving them waiting still tells them nothing, since nobody gains their training then, and they are not on Today's waiting list. The other way: tell them nothing (the first build, which the review refused: the Owner could take a trainee and read their training silently). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d1b, clients waiting on the owner's Today.** A list of its own beside Needs you (`waitingForCoach`, the owner's alone: who, since when, who coached them before), not an attention kind, so the rule stays at v9 and N9d2 can put it first, as C8 asks. The other way: a "Waiting for a coach" kind in Needs you. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d1b, This month during a wait.** What happens while a client waits (money received, a plan or pack booked, a client started or ended) counts for nobody, never for the coach who left before the wait, and the owner's page gains a No coach line beside the team's rows so they still add up (SUB-018 v3); a session always counts for who held it. The other way: count it for the owner, which C8's "never automatically" argues against. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d1b, the Team page.** Each member's client count leaves the waiting clients out; the list gains no "waiting" count, since its answer is a plain list the web reads as it is, and the owner's Today carries the waiting clients. The other way: a waiting count on the Team page. Recommendation: N9d2's Team sentence can point to Today.
- **N9d1b, a departed coach's pending invitations.** They name no coach and a client who accepts one lands waiting; nothing gives a pending invitation a coach before it is accepted. The other way: a route to give a pending invitation a coach. Recommendation: wait; the owner assigns the client once they join.
- **N9d1b, until N9d2.** The web API client is not regenerated, so the screens still say the old things: removing a coach promises "Their clients move to you" and the coach's Leave page "Your clients move to the owner", a waiting client shows a blank coach on Clients (an empty option in the coach chip) and "Coached by" with no name on their page, the coach panel says "The current coach loses access" and shows only with more than one member, This month ignores the No coach line, the owner's Today ignores the waiting list, and the two new notices open nothing; the live spec `e2e/live/team-reassign.e2e.ts` still expects the owner after a resignation and a removal. The server is right meanwhile. Recommendation: build N9d2 next, as the plan's order says.
- **N9d1b, the technical choices.** No new history reason (taking a waiting client is `Reassigned` from nobody, so the web's reason words still cover every entry); `ForDeparture` for a departure's entry and `ForChange` naming a coach; the database refuses a new departure entry that names anybody and a released record with no keeper; the migration's way back refuses while anything waited; the removal's answer keeps its field names (`reassignedClientCount`, `reassignedInvitationCount`, now the clients and invitations left waiting) because the web reads them; a client's details carry an empty coach name while waiting (a null would print "Coached by null" on today's web) while the Clients list carries none; the Today list costs one tagged read, only when somebody waits.
- **N9d2, Today's waiting list.** A panel of its own above Needs you, only while somebody waits: "Waiting for a coach" with its count, "Nobody sees their training until you give them a coach. Their plan keeps running and they can still log.", each row "Since Sat 14 Nov · Coached by Lea Khoury before" (or "No coach yet") and "Choose a coach", which opens the coach dialog on the client's Service & access (`?action=coach`) and is Today's one filled button while anybody waits. The other way: a dialog on Today itself. Recommendation: keep: one dialog, its words and its history in one place.
- **N9d2, another coach's client on the owner's Today (N9d1a's line).** Kept in Needs you with its sentence, a line naming who follows up ("Lea Khoury reviews it." or "reviews them.", "Lea Khoury follows up.", for a waiting client "Their new coach follows up once you choose one.") and "See client", which opens the client's page; never the filled button; the greeting still counts these clients as having "something for you". The other way: leave them out of the owner's Needs you, or group them under each coach. Recommendation: read it on the pictures (`one-owner-today-*`); grouping is the next step if it crowds the owner's own work.
- **N9d2, Today's This month card.** A "No coach" line under Coaches for the clients no member holds (every client less the members' rows, so a house trainee with no coach counts there though not on the waiting list). The other way: the rows left short of the total. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the closed side of a client's page.** For a client the owner does not coach the tabs are Overview and Service & access, a private tab reached by its address shows the same panel instead, and the Overview opens with it: "Lea Khoury coaches Maya" / "As the owner you see Maya's plan, payments and sessions. Their training, nutrition, check-ins and progress open only to their coach." with Service & access; a waiting client "Maya is waiting for a coach" with Choose a coach; a former client the owner did not coach keeps its Former client panel with "Their training, check-ins and progress stay closed: you did not coach them when coaching ended." The other way: the private tabs shown, each saying it is closed. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the Overview of another coach's client.** Its Needs you rows say who acts and offer no button; a blocked client reads "Maya's access is blocked until you unblock them."; the coach notes card (now the coach's alone) says "Only you see these, as Maya's coach.", and "Only you see these." on a former client. The other way: "See client" as on Today, which would point at the page itself. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, choosing a coach for a waiting client.** Nothing is chosen until the owner picks (C8: never the owner automatically), the team's coaches by name and You last; the sentence follows the choice ("The coach you choose sees Maya and all their history right away.", "You become Maya's coach and see their training, nutrition, check-ins and progress right away.") and ends "Maya is told of the change.", since a client's details do not say whether Maya is a self-directed trainee, whose notice has words of its own. The other way: the first choice chosen, as for a reassignment. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, an owner alone.** The coach panel shows for a waiting client even with nobody else on the team, so the owner can take them; after the save it keeps the coach line and "You now coach Maya." (focus there) with no button. The other way: hide the panel once nothing else can be chosen. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the coach history's words.** An entry naming nobody reads "Waiting for a coach" as its coach, and the entry that ends a wait "after waiting for a coach". The other way: "nobody". Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, Clients.** A waiting row reads "Waiting for a coach" where "Coached by" goes; the Coach chip ends with "Waiting for a coach" (`?coach=waiting`, the chip then reading just that, with empty words of its own); a row the owner does not coach leaves its Last activity out; a waiting self-directed trainee reads the same. The other way: a status pill for waiting. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the Team page and Leave.** Team: "You see every client's plan, payments and sessions, and their training, nutrition, check-ins and progress only when you coach them yourself. …" and "When a coach leaves, their clients wait for a coach. Clients lists them under Waiting for a coach, so you can give each one a coach, yourself included."; the remove dialog and its toast say the clients wait. Leave: "… The owner is told, so they can give each one a coach." Clients, not Today, since Today's list leaves self-directed trainees out (CLI-020), and "the owner is told" since a trainee is not told of a departure (CLI-014). The other way: name Today, with the house's own words. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, This month's No coach line.** A row before Total, "No coach · While clients waited for a coach", with the table shown even when the owner is the only coach, and "While a client waits for a coach, it counts on the No coach line." only while the line shows. The other way: the sentence always. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the notices.** "You have a new coach" and "Who can see your training changed" open Today with the clients icon; the owner's undelivered list names them "A new coach for a client" and "A change of who sees a trainee's training", and CoachDeparted "A client's coach leaving". The other way: open Me. Recommendation: keep: the notice says Today.
- **N9d2, the client's own screens while waiting.** Today's coach chip reads "Waiting for a coach" (words only, two lines on a phone), "Your coaching space will give you a new coach, who will put your program here." where no program runs, and Message your coach and Contact your coach wait for the coach read and stay hidden while waiting; Me's card "Waiting for a new coach" / "Your coaching space will tell you when you have one."; a self-directed trainee never reads them. The other way: the links shown as soon as Messaging allows, as before. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, the privacy lines a client reads.** "Only you, your coach and the owner of <space> can see these answers." became "Only you and your coach can see these answers." on the intake, likewise the check-in flow's, and the photo sheet's "Your coach can see these photos…", true since N9d1a. They are the app's own lines, not the lawyer's privacy wording. Recommendation: keep; the lawyer may reword them with the rest.
- **N9d2, the technical choices.** The web API client regenerated; `mayCoach !== false` wherever it is read (a missing value is the old rule); the closed side decided from the record before any private request (`side: 'private'` on the client's private routes and the outlet replaced, pinned against the real routes; `confirmPageLeave` where a page may never be created); one filled button on Today (`primaryAction`); `?action=coach` through the coach panel's `openRequested`; the check-ins to review keep their coach line as a guard the server no longer reaches (CHK-014). Recommendation: keep.
- **N9d2, outside the row: unused space names.** The intake's and the check-in flow's `spaceName` inputs are unused now that their privacy lines name no owner; removing them touches their parent pages. Recommendation: a one-line clean-up in a later web row.
- **N9c5a, the split.** N9c5 was split by its chat into N9c5a (the server, built) and N9c5b (the screens, left Open), as N9c1 and N9c2, N9c3 and N9c4 and N9d1 and N9d2 were: the server alone touched about twenty files and three test files of its own. The other way: one chat for both. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, a place is held from the booking.** The row's default: an ask holds no place; a booked Max plan holds one from its booking until it ends, whether it runs, is paused or is booked to start later, and a trainee holding one keeps it for their next pass; a cancelled or ended plan frees it, and fewer places never end a running plan, they only refuse new bookings until enough end. The other way: a place held from the ask, which lets an ask that is never paid keep a place. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, the line and paying.** The open Max asks of trainees holding no place stand in the order asked; an ask's place is open when its turn is within the places free. "I've paid" is refused until the ask's place is open (`house_max_place_not_open`), so nobody sends money for a place that is not there, and once said it holds the place until the ask is booked or closed, so neither a later booking nor the end of a running pass takes it (the review's finding); an earlier ask whose money is never said sent holds the turn to pay until the staff close it, and the booking itself keeps to the count, not the turn. A trainee who has left the house drops out of the line; while Max is closed no open ask reads as having a place; a Max option reads "place open" only when an ask made now, at the back of the line, would have one; and "I've paid" said again with the same details still answers as before. The other way: anyone pays at once and the staff book in the order the money comes. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, Max only from an ask.** The coaches' ordinary Start a plan and Renew refuse a Max plan in the house (`house_max_by_ask`), so its places are kept; Pro+ is left as it was. The other way: the staff book Max by hand too, with the places counted there. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, Max's four features.** A Max plan's options must cover Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging, all four (the row's rule: what a coach's client gets); any other set is refused when the plan is made or an option added. The other way: the staff tick what they like. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, the words.** "Max isn't taking anyone right now. Your ask is kept; don't send money until it opens again." (the trainee, Max closed), "Max is closed: TB Gym's settings give it no places. Book this ask once Max opens again, or close it." (the staff), "Every place on Max is taken. Book this ask once a place opens, or close it.", "A Max place isn't open for you yet. Your ask is kept in line; don't send money until a place opens.", "Max is booked from the trainee's ask, so its places are kept.", "A Max plan covers Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging, all four.", the House page's refusal "Enter a whole number of places from 0 to 1,000, or leave it empty. Empty or 0 keeps Max closed." and "Health answers open on Max, TB Gym's coaching. Your other answers can still be saved." Recommendation: keep until the partners write Max's own words.
- **N9c5a, coached while Max grants.** Read on the server from the house's plans each time and never stored: a Max plan that is paused, cancelled, unpaid or not yet started, a blocked trainee and a house that is no longer active all leave the trainee their own coach; the day Max ends their items leave Coach Today at the house's midnight with nobody told. The other way: a stored flag the booking sets and a sweep clears. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, no plan lines for a house trainee on Today.** A Max trainee raises what a coach's client raises, without Plan ending soon or an asked renewal (a house plan is renewed by its own ask, and the coaches' Renew is refused for Max); a session pack sold in the house would still say its sessions are running out. The other way: the plan lines too, with a link to the house's ask. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, a Max trainee with no coach.** A Max booking of a trainee waiting for a coach is allowed (money is the business side), and the trainee is then on the Owner's waiting list with Choose a coach. The other way: refuse the booking until a coach is chosen. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, what stays the house's on Max.** A Max trainee keeps the pool, workouts of their own, a session started early in its week and the house's asks; the programs page's Start and typing their own max stay Pro+'s (`house_program_needs_pass`), so their coach assigns the program and types the maxes. One ask is open at a time across the tiers, so a trainee waiting in Max's line asks for Pro+ only once the staff close that ask. The other way: let a waiting trainee hold a Pro+ ask beside it. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, the Health step's edges.** Off Max a health answer added or changed is refused; one kept as it is or emptied passes, from the trainee or the staff; answers saved on Max are kept when it ends and read by the trainee and their coach; a paid Max that starts after a running Pro+ opens the step on its first day; the released, set-up and age refusals come first, and an answer too long is still the form's refusal. The other way: emptying refused too, or the answers hidden once Max ends. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5a, outside the row: the health consent on Max.** A trainee is never asked the health intake consent (ADR 0041), Max included; whether moving onto Max should ask it is the lawyer's question with the consumer terms. Not built. Recommendation: put it on the lawyer's list before Max is switched on.
- **N9c5b, a full Max on My plan.** While every place is taken, the Max plan reads "Every place on Max is taken right now. You can still ask: your ask waits in line, and you send money only once a place opens for you." under what it includes, and its ask sheet says the ask waits in line, in the order asked, and not to send money yet; while Max is closed the plan is not listed at all (the server's read). The other way: no Ask while Max is full. Recommendation: keep; asks are the count that says Max is wanted.
- **N9c5b, a Max ask waiting for its place.** The trainee's card reads "Waiting for a place" and N9c5a's sentence ("A Max place isn’t open for you yet. Your ask is kept in line; don’t send money until a place opens.") and shows no Whish number, Copy, "If you already sent the money" or I've paid; once its place opens it reads as Pro+'s with "A place on Max is open for you now." first. Nobody tells the trainee when a place opens: they see it on My plan. The other way: an in-app notice when an ask's place opens, which needs the server. Recommendation: that notice as a small server row before Max is switched on.
- **N9c5b, the House page's name for Max's places.** "Trainees on Max at a time", with "A whole number from 0 to 1,000. Empty or 0 keeps Max closed. A booked Max plan keeps its place until it ends.", the value shown as a bare number, and a toast that says when Max closes (0 or empty). The other way: "Max places". Recommendation: keep, so it is never read as the week's sign-ups.
- **N9c5b, the staff's turn lines.** On a current trainee's open Max ask: "Number <n> in line for Max. A place is open, so <name> can pay.", "Number <n> in line for Max. No place is open for this ask yet, so <name>’s page says not to send money.", "<name> holds a Max place." or "Max is closed: TB Gym’s settings give it no places."; none on a Pro+ ask, an ended ask or a former trainee's. Book plan stays open whatever the turn, since the server books by free places (N9c5a), and its sheet warns when the ask has no place and no money was said sent. The other way: Book plan for the first in line alone. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5b, Renew and Start a plan with Max.** A Max plan never gets the filled Renew, its Manage plan Renew is disabled with "Max is booked from the trainee’s ask, so its places are kept.", and neither the client page's header nor `?renew=` offers to renew it; Start a plan still lists Max's options and shows the server's refusal (`house_max_by_ask`) if one is chosen. The other way: Max left out of Start a plan's choices. Recommendation: leave it out in the small web row N9c2's line asks for on Free's buttons.
- **N9c5b, a Max plan's price options.** Add price option on a Max plan starts with Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging ticked and locked and Resources off and locked, with "A Max plan covers Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging, all four."; the server's refusal of another set is shown under What's included. Recommendation: keep.
- **N9c5b, Assign program and Free.** Free is never offered as the plan a program goes under, so a Max trainee's first choice is their Max plan; a trainee on Free alone gets no Assign program and reads "<name> is on Free alone, which covers their own workouts and no program.
